## Wave 11 — decisions (D107–D115, DECIDED with two verify rounds in hand; the ARCHITECTURAL CRUX opens — Cloud execution isolation, smallest honest increment)

Wave 11 opens the P1 runner (`task-fe78ccb664308739`) with the minimal provable slice: the execution-profile
seam + a one-shot sandbox-runner shim + ONE bogus-key isolated turn proven end-to-end. Every decision below is
grounded in run output recorded in `connectors-wave-11-2026-07-16`. Verify killed two premises: there is NO
stdin path into a running Vercel Sandbox (SDK types AND live CLI probes agree), and the inherited Elixir red
is no longer `runtime_target`.

- **D107 [DECIDED — the profile switch is a config-keyed resolver in `ClaudeChat.command/2`, NOT a third `execution_target`.]** New app config `:execution_profile` under `:claude_chat` (`:self_hosted` default | `:cloud`; greenfield — grep-proven zero references anywhere). `command/2` (claude_chat.ex:100) already branches config-override-vs-default; `:cloud` adds ONE branch returning `{sandbox_runner(), cloud_build_args(mode, opts)}`. Self-hosted stays BYTE-IDENTICAL (run-proven: no config ⇒ `EXE=claude` + the full interactive argv, `--print --verbose --input-format stream-json …`). REJECTED: a third `execution_target` enum value (persisted per-session 2-value enum `~w(managed registered_host)` session.ex:27 — migration-adjacent, and the WRONG AXIS: profile is an instance/deployment property, not a per-session user choice) and a new `Runtime.open` clause (duplicates the managed dispatch, session.ex blast radius). A cloud session keeps `execution_target=managed`. Port.open (:908), spawn_env (:482) and the Session GenServer are UNTOUCHED — the shim rides the SAME `{exe,args,env,cwd}→NDJSON` contract (D10 verbatim).
- **D108 [DECIDED — one-shot LOCK; the shim buffers ITS OWN stdin, so Session spawn timing is untouched.]** Interactive stdin into a sandbox is IMPOSSIBLE: `@vercel/sandbox@2.6.1` has zero stdin surface (grep of shipped `.d.ts` = NO_STDIN_MATCH; `Command` has no write(), kill() is signals-only); live CLI probes: piped stdin DROPPED (`wc -c` read 0 of 6 piped bytes), `--interactive` refuses non-TTY. But the SHIM is a local child — Elixir's Port stdin write reaches it fine. So the shim: buffer the FIRST complete `{"type":"user",…}` frame from local stdin → create the sandbox → write the frame to an in-sandbox file (base64-embedded exec; file-redirect run-proven) → run ONE-SHOT `claude --input-format stream-json --output-format stream-json < file` (input-mode one-shot run-proven to emit the identical 3-frame vocabulary as `-p` mode) → stream stdout NDJSON verbatim → exit with claude's code (bogus-key path exits 1, proven). ONE turn per cloud session this increment; mid-turn control frames (interrupt/set_permission_mode/control_response) are logged+dropped by the shim; interactive multi-turn = named follow-on (needs a PTY, undocumented).
- **D109 [DECIDED — the cloud argv contract.]** KEEP `--input-format stream-json --output-format stream-json --include-partial-messages --verbose --permission-mode <mode>`. DROP `--permission-prompt-tool stdio` (no channel to answer asks one-shot; safe — the bogus-key 401 fires before any tool ask). DROP mcp args ENTIRELY (`--strict-mcp-config` + a host-written `--mcp-config` path HARD-fails in-sandbox with ZERO NDJSON output — run-proven; in-sandbox connector tools = named follow-on). NEVER emit bypass args from the cloud builder — structurally unreachable, pinned by test (a down-payment on D24 knob 1, NOT the 5-knob build). The workspace id rides the shim argv (e.g. `--workspace <id> --` before the claude args); the builder picks exact flag names, the test pins presence.
- **D110 [DECIDED — fail-closed workspace principal at spawn.]** `Runtime.Claude.start_session` (claude.ex:56-67) silently DROPS the `workspace_id` the recorder already threads (recorder.ex:139) — one `Map.put` narrowing fixes it into `session_opts`. The cloud resolver then HARD-FAILS on nil/`:global` workspace (registered_host's `Map.fetch!` shape, runtime.ex:349 — never the fail-soft mcp empty-list pattern), pinned by a named ExUnit test: today the managed path would spawn a nil-workspace turn without complaint (fail-open by omission, run-proven unpinned by any test). This wave workspace_id only TAGS the sandbox; per-workspace KEY sourcing stays D9-gated (`task-5766dc5ca985ddc8`). The stub bogus key comes from the shim's own env/config ONLY — never `Barkpark.Secrets` (flat instance-global), never the connector seal (Elixir-opaque by design, D38).
- **D111 [DECIDED — shim = dependency-free Node over the `vercel` CLI; teardown = explicit-remove-on-EOF + SIGTERM trap + mandatory `--timeout`.]** `Port.close` delivers NO signal to the child — run-proven on all three spawn shapes (children SURVIVED close and ran to natural completion; claude_chat.ex:1080's EOF comment is the CORRECT model, deploy_runner.ex:409's "terminates the program" comment is empirically WRONG). An Elixir Port has zero OS relationship to a remote microVM — nothing tears a sandbox down for free. Contract: (1) stdin-EOF watcher → explicit `vercel sandbox remove` (REMOVE, not stop — stopped sandboxes accrue snapshot storage against a 15GB Hobby lifetime cap); (2) SIGTERM/SIGINT/exit trap → same teardown; (3) EVERY create passes a bounded `--timeout` (10m ceiling); (4) every sandbox tagged `purpose=cloud-turn` + workspace (reaper-sweepable via `sandbox ls --tag`); (5) pin `--scope guerrilla` (run-proven Pro team; the DEFAULT scope `frikk-jarls-projects` is HOBBY — 10 concurrent, 45min cap, 30-DAY CREATION PAUSE on quota overrun = an outage, not a bill); (6) env-clamped concurrency. CLI not SDK this increment (the CLI is what D10/D23 proved under frikk's auth; SDK + token auth = named follow-on riding the deploy human gate).
- **D112 [DECIDED — hook/cwd isolation is a REQUIRED property of the cloud turn.]** Run-proven: a dirty HOME or a cwd with `.claude/settings.json` leaks 8–10 SessionStart hook frames (multi-KB doc dumps; hooks SHELL OUT) into the stdout NDJSON before init; the committed fixture's own provenance line discloses hand-stripping them. The shim runs claude with an isolated HOME + clean cwd inside the sandbox; the image is a clean npm install, never baked from an operator checkout. Also pinned: CLI warnings go to STDERR (stdout stays pure NDJSON in every mode tested); `parse_chunk` tolerates stray frames (no crash risk) but customer-facing streams must not carry hook payloads.
- **D113 [DECIDED — the honest three-leg proof stack; the live turn stays human-gated.]** (a) PURE ExUnit: `command/2` under `:cloud` returns the shim + the D109 argv; the default profile byte-identical. (b) END-TO-END ExUnit with a chmod+x FAKE shim (argv_echo/env_dump precedent, claude_chat_test.exs:1509/:1528): real Port.open through Session, a canned bogus-key result frame flows the SAME parse_chunk→dispatch pipeline, asserted via `auth_failure?/1`'s fields (`is_error:true`, `api_error_status:401`) — zero live sandbox, CI-green. (c) COMMITTED live harness `scripts/connectors/w11-isolated-turn-proof.sh` (d10 idioms verbatim: `--check` free for CI, EXIT-trap teardown, `--tag`, `--timeout`): real sandbox (plain node24 + npm-installed claude — latency is D10's ledger, NOT this proof's bar; the D10 snapshot is deleted), real env-injection of a BOGUS key, one isolated turn, asserts the terminal frame `type:result is_error:true api_error_status:401 "Invalid API key…"` (field-diffed byte-identical to the committed fixture's non-volatile fields, run-proven this round), then `vercel sandbox ls` shows zero orphans. The LIVE model turn (real ANTHROPIC key) and the real per-workspace Vercel deploy are NAMED human gates (`connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn`) — deliver up to them, never fabricate.
- **D114 [CORRECTED — merge ceiling re-derived: `runtime_target` is GONE; the sole named red is the QueueTest CTE flake.]** 0b303bc59 fixed deploy_runner (run-proven absent from its own CI run). The freshest completed main run (29458659149) fails EXACTLY ONE test: `Barkpark.Tasks.QueueTest` "dependency plan hashes a one-time done-task scan" (queue_test.exs:462) — a self-documented planner-choice FLAKE (the source comment names the live flake; it recurred independently on a pre-fix run 5h earlier). MERGE CONTRACT: the failing set at merge must be exactly `{that QueueTest flake}` OR empty — nothing new; the lead re-derives from the freshest completed run immediately before merge (only N=1 post-fix sample existed at Decide; run 29460587793 was still in-flight). Isolation law unchanged: never touch paper components / golden fixtures / deploy_runner / any `/api` route; the .ex diff stays Studio-token-clean (no hex). Format advisory-red persists (inherited, non-blocking).
- **D115 [DECIDED — scope-outs by name + honest crux-criteria mapping.]** OUT this wave: the D24 5-knob bypass reversal (only the never-emits-bypass argv test rides along); D22 warm pool; D9 per-workspace keys; multi-workspace routing; in-sandbox MCP tools; interactive multi-turn; SDK/token auth; the live Vercel deploy; a sandbox reaper cron. ONE slice, ONE PR, opus. Paper: `connectors-wave-11-2026-07-16`.

## Wave 12 — decisions (D116–D125, DECIDED with two verify rounds in hand; the D24 permission reversal lands at the ARGV/POLICY layer)

Wave 12 hardens W11's wired `:cloud` profile into D24's security-correct posture, proven at the layer that is
honestly provable without the live deploy — the LAUNCH CONTRACT (argv + policy). The decisive verify finding
KILLS the "restrictive permission-mode = safety" intuition: with the exact cloud argv (no
`--permission-prompt-tool`, one-shot), EVERY `--permission-mode` — `plan` included — AUTO-APPROVED and
EXECUTED a Bash tool call on a real logged-in `claude` 2.1.211 (all five modes probed; `tool_results`
`W12PROBE`, `permission_denials=[]`; clean HOME/projects verified — genuine headless behavior, not a saved
allowlist). Unattended tool safety therefore comes from tool REMOVAL and the sandbox boundary, never from the
mode. All decisions grounded in run output recorded in `connectors-wave-12-2026-07-16`.

- **D116 [DECIDED+CORRECTED — knob 1 is a VALIDITY clamp, never a safety claim.]** New `@cloud_modes = @modes -- ["bypassPermissions"]`; `cloud_claude_args` clamps through `CloudPolicy.cloud_permission_mode/1` (member → verbatim; anything else, bypass included → `"plan"`, mirroring the self-hosted fail-closed idiom). The authoritative valid set is the CLI's own reject list (probed: `acceptEdits, auto, bypassPermissions, manual, dontAsk, plan`; `manual` normalizes to internal `default`). The clamp completes knob 1 beyond W11's never-emits-bypass down-payment, but per the probe above it MUST NOT be documented as tool confinement — mode does not gate tools headless.
- **D117 [DECIDED — knob 2 is THE client-side enforcement: triple belt from ONE policy owner.]** New module `Barkpark.Connectors.CloudPolicy` is the single legible answer to "exactly what may a Cloud turn do", and `cloud_claude_args/…` emits all three belts from it: (a) `--tools ""` — removes ALL built-in tools (probe: init frame `has_Bash=False`; CLI help verbatim: `"" to disable all tools`); MCP tools survive `--tools ""`, but the cloud path has NO mcp args (D109) + isolated HOME (D112), so none exist in-sandbox; (b) `--disallowedTools <deny set>` — probe-proven to structurally remove a tool without any bridge, never fails open; deny set `@cloud_disallowed_tools = ["Bash","Edit","Write","Read","NotebookEdit","WebFetch","WebSearch","Task"]` (the host-FS/exec/network built-ins); (c) `--settings '{"permissions":{"deny":[...]}}'` as a JSON STRING argv (probe: loads into the `flagSettings` scope; permission rules MERGE across scopes, deny beats allow). REJECTED: a shim-written settings.json FILE — probe proved a malformed settings file FAILS OPEN SILENTLY under `-p` (truncated JSON → deny never loads, zero surfaced error; `--help` documents the silent ignore), so the file vector is strictly weaker than argv; the JSON string is built with `Jason.encode!` from the SAME list as (b), and a test pins argv-deny == settings-deny (no drift). All three flags probe-proven to parse together with `--permission-mode`.
- **D118 [DECIDED — knob 3 ships its POLICY half only; the wiring stays with the two existing tasks.]** `CloudPolicy.connector_tool_providers/1` = `Catalog.installs_for_workspace/1` (catalog.ex:411, real cross-schema Ecto read, D72's by-workspace gap CLOSED by PR #3366) composed with `Catalog.direction/1 == :tool` — a pure function, DB-backed unit test (the test bootstrap already creates `chat_bridge.connector_installs`, D54). The literal charter phrase `bp mcp serve --tools <subset>` does not exist (Go flag is `tasks|all` only) and connector tools are wired as separate per-provider mcpServers entries — the implementation is ALREADY owned by `task-0c8b9214b7962152` (Go subset flag) + `connectors-cloud-sandbox-mcp-tools` (in-sandbox wiring); NO third task gets filed.
- **D119 [DECIDED — knob 4 (grant-scoped token mint) scoped OUT by name.]** D9-blocked (run-secrets is flat instance-global; per-workspace scoping = `task-5766dc5ca985ddc8`). Ships as a named criterion on that task, zero code this wave.
- **D120 [DECIDED — knob 5 is a WRITTEN policy, stated where the code lives.]** The `CloudPolicy` moduledoc states verbatim: a headless one-shot cloud turn has NO interactive permission gate; with the cloud argv every permission-mode auto-approves tool calls (probed all five, claude 2.1.211 — VERSION-OBSERVED, not contractual; pin the version in the text); unattended tool safety comes from (1) the Firecracker sandbox boundary, (2) the key-gate (the current path 401s before any ask — W11), (3) knob-2 tool REMOVAL — never from the mode. D65's bare `-p` auto-DENY datapoint was MCP-specific and does NOT generalize (built-in Bash auto-RAN in all modes). Live enforcement observation rides `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn`.
- **D121 [DECIDED — the NEW boundary fix: the LOCAL `vercel` CLI child gets an explicit env allowlist.]** Verify found the real leak the wish missed: host→sandbox is already narrow (capture-proven EXACTLY one `--env ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` on the turn exec), but `run()` (cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs:114-118) spawns the LOCAL `vercel` child with FULL env inherit — a planted `HOST_SECRET` and the minted per-session `BARKPARK_API_TOKEN` were captured verbatim in the fake-vercel child's env. Fix: explicit allowlist on every local `vercel` spawn — `HOME`, `PATH`, `TMPDIR` + `VERCEL_*`-prefixed (auth: `VERCEL_AUTH_TOKEN`/`VERCEL_OIDC_TOKEN` confirmed read from `--help`; org/project ids unconfirmed → prefix-allow); `BARKPARK_*` and `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` never reach the vercel child (the shim reads `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` from its OWN env to build the one `--env` flag). The remote boundary needs no change: `vercel sandbox exec` forwards ONLY explicit `--env` pairs (CLI help + SDK docs; fork explicitly does NOT copy env).
- **D122 [DECIDED — the shim proof is BLACK-BOX subprocess, and it finally rides CI.]** `CLOUD_SANDBOX_VERCEL_BIN` → fake capture script drives the REAL runner end-to-end with zero shim refactor (run-proven this round: create→exec→remove lifecycle, all four confinement assertions checkable from captures; the import-safety refactor is NOT needed). `scripts/connectors/**` is currently INVISIBLE to every CI path filter — `.github/workflows/connectors.yml` gains `scripts/connectors/**` in its `paths` and a step running the new `w12-shim-confinement-proof.sh`. The proof script must not depend on GNU `timeout` (absent on darwin) and must locate the turn exec by its `-lc` argv token, never by capture-file ordering (the snapshot path shifts numbering).
- **D123 [DECIDED — D110 gets its Recorder-level end-to-end assertion.]** All existing D110 tests stop at `Runtime.Claude`/`ClaudeChat`; the two production entry points (LiveView chat_live.ex:3698, chat_controller.ex:534) thread `workspace_id` correctly but NO test crosses `Recorder.ensure` for `:cloud`. W12 adds one: `Recorder.ensure` under `:cloud` with a nil workspace fails closed (no spawn), through the real Session pipeline with the existing fake-shim harness.
- **D124 [CORRECTED — merge ceiling re-derived and WIDENED to a rotating 3-shape flake population.]** W11's own merge-commit run (29464502751 on 78a94ee11) failed EXACTLY the QueueTest `ready/1` flake (11148 tests, 1 failure); the freshest completed run at Decide (29465097161) failed a THIRD shape the digest never catalogued — `RuntimeAdmissionTest` "owner termination releases capacity without a decrement race" (timing test by construction, studio_chat/, unrelated to connectors); EnginePerfTest recompute/1 confirmed on 29463949074; a fully-green run (29463951803) sits between them — genuinely intermittent. MERGE CONTRACT: failing set at merge ⊆ {QueueTest ready/1 dependency-plan hash; EnginePerfTest recompute/1 complexity floor; RuntimeAdmissionTest owner-termination race} OR empty, RE-DERIVED from the freshest completed main run immediately pre-merge (the set has proven to shift run-to-run); the wave's own new tests 100% green; Format advisory-red inherited; Prod compile gate green. Isolation law unchanged (no paper-components/golden/deploy_runner/openapi/api-routes/Studio hex).
- **D125 [DECIDED — scope-outs by name + honest crux mapping.]** OUT this wave: knob-3 wiring (owned by the two existing tasks, D118); knob-4 code (D9, D119); the LIVE tool-denial observation and settings deny-beats-allow runtime resolution (auth-gated — both named onto `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn`, never fabricated); interactive multi-turn; SDK auth; warm pool. Newly filed backlog: `connectors-cloud-claude-version-pin` (permission semantics are version-observed on 2.1.211; the sandbox npm-installs claude fresh — pin/assert the in-sandbox version), `connectors-scrubbed-env-selfaudit-widen` (the denylist self-audit only scans config/runtime.exs; ANTHROPIC_API_KEY-style reads in api/lib are invisible to it). Crux `task-fe78ccb664308739` criterion 2 ("knobs 1,2,4,5 built"): W12 delivers knobs 1, 2, 5 + knob 3's policy half; knob 4 stays D9-gated — stamp EVIDENCE on criterion 2 but leave it unmet until knob 4 lands or the criterion is honestly amended.

## Wave 13 — decisions (D126–D133, DECIDED with two verify rounds in hand; knob-3 wiring — the locked :cloud profile becomes FUNCTIONAL at the config layer)

Wave 13 closes knob-3's wiring half on top of W12's CloudPolicy policy half (D118): a Cloud workspace with
tool connectors installed launches a turn whose sandboxed claude is CONFIGURED with exactly those connector
MCP servers — and the W12 deny belt stays byte-intact. Every fork below closed with run output recorded in
`connectors-wave-13-2026-07-16`. The decisive verify corrections: `bp mcp serve` structurally CANNOT serve
connector tools (it bridges bp's OWN manifest nouns; github/linear are Catalog rows, not manifest nouns —
fixture grep = 0), and live in-sandbox serving is blocked by TWO walls beyond the human gate (egress lock +
no sandbox-viable credential). The wave's honest ceiling is the argv/config layer — exactly the wish's scope.

- **D126 [DECIDED — the mcpServers referent: per-provider HTTP descriptor entries, NEVER a stdio bp entry.]** The cloud mcpServers map = one `{"type":"http","url":<descriptor.url>}` entry per workspace tool-install (github→`api.githubcopilot.com/mcp/`, linear→`mcp.linear.app/mcp` — both toolDescriptors REAL, linear.ts:104/:132, github.ts:80/:199). URL stays single-sourced in Node: the Elixir HOST fetches descriptors via the existing `BridgeClient.fetch_tool_descriptors` loopback (host-side loopback works; fail-soft to `[]` — no bridge/secret ⇒ no entries, honest empty, a deploy precondition not a bug). REJECTED: an Elixir-static provider→url map (forks the URL out of its single Node owner, contra D75's no-endpoint law); a stdio `bp mcp serve` entry for connectors (bp binary ABSENT in-sandbox; the Go bridge walks `m.Commands` with zero connector concept — run-proven). `headersHelper` is STRIPPED from every cloud entry (it is a curl to the HOST loopback, dead across Firecracker — D71's precondition does not exist for :cloud); entries ship credential-less this wave.
- **D127 [DECIDED — flag ownership: the SHIM owns all mcp argv; 0 installs ⇒ byte-identical W12 argv.]** Elixir emits ONE new shim-own flag `--mcp-config-b64 <base64(mcpServers json)>` BEFORE the `--` separator (parseArgv breaks at `--`; anything after is handed to claude verbatim — probe-proven), and ONLY when ≥1 tool-direction install exists; with 0 installs the cloud argv is BYTE-IDENTICAL to W12 (no flag at all — the existing exact-argv pin survives as the 0-install row). The shim, when the flag is present: adds one `printf '%s' <b64> | base64 -d > /tmp/bp-cloud-mcp.json` line to `inSandboxScript` AFTER the frame line and BEFORE the exec, and appends `--mcp-config /tmp/bp-cloud-mcp.json --strict-mcp-config` to the claude command. D109's host-path finding SURVIVES (a host-written path still hard-fails; the config is materialized IN-VM); D109's "no mcp args" pin is AMENDED, not preserved. RUN-PROVEN at verify: this exact shape trips NONE of w12-shim-confinement-proof.sh's four assertions (4/4 PASS, exit 0) and keeps EXACTLY one `--env` pair — a second `--env` (e.g. a credential) WOULD break the pin; credentials never ride `--env`. `--strict-mcp-config` restricts SOURCES not COUNT (D69), so the 0/1/2 table is mechanically clean.
- **D128 [DECIDED — CloudPolicy stays the ONE :cloud posture owner; it gains the pure config-emission half.]** New pure function in `CloudPolicy` (e.g. `cloud_mcp_servers(installs, descriptors)`) composing `connector_tool_providers/1` (D118, shipped) with the host-fetched descriptors: keep only descriptors whose provider has a tool-direction install, emit `%{provider => %{"type"=>"http","url"=>url}}`, DROP headersHelper and anything malformed (fail-closed, reuse the `mcp_config/2` fold idiom incl. `Map.put_new` reserved-key guard). `claude_chat.ex` threads workspace + descriptors into `cloud_build_args/2` and base64-encodes — the policy module never fetches, the chat module never decides. Tests stay pure: descriptors are INJECTED in ExUnit (no live bridge needed); the bridge fetch is the same host-side idiom `setup_mcp` already uses.
- **D129 [DECIDED — Go `--tools` vocabulary: comma-separated NOUN list; bridge-only; shadow skip stays; parse stays syntactic.]** `--tools <noun,noun>` keyed on `Command.Noun` (the bridge's own naming key, mcp_bridge.go:130). A subset registers BRIDGE tools ONLY (never the curated task eight — `tasks` already means those); `bridgeShadowedIDs` skip STAYS active (inert for connector nouns, preserves never-double-expose; `--tools task` correctly yields zero). Parse-time validation is SYNTACTIC only: reserve `tasks`/`all`, reject empty comma tokens; noun EXISTENCE is NOT validated (the manifest loads after parse; an absent noun degrades to zero tools = the honest 0-install behavior, never a usage error). `parseMCPServeArgs` grows a subset return (small signature ripple confined to mcp_serve.go/_test.go). RUN-PROVEN at verify with a throwaway probe: `github,linear` → exactly 3 tools, `github` → 2, empty → 0, `task` → 0 (shadowed). HONESTY LAW: github/linear are NOT bp manifest nouns (fixture nouns grep = 0) — the flag is a GENERAL noun-subset filter proven with a SYNTHETIC manifest; it is NOT the transport by which cloud claude reaches connectors (that is D126's HTTP entries). Tests, docs, and the ledger must never claim otherwise.
- **D130 [DECIDED — the test amend/preserve map, surgical.]** AMEND `claude_chat_test.exs:897-924` (exact-argv pin) by SPLITTING into a 0-install row (byte-identical W12 argv — KEEP the exact-equality pin) and ≥1-install rows (new: `--mcp-config-b64` present pre-`--`, its decode == exactly the scoped map). PRESERVE `:926-938`'s host-path refutes VERBATIM for the ELIXIR-emitted argv — `--mcp-config`, `--strict-mcp-config`, and `/host/mcp.json` never appear in Elixir's args under D127 (both flags are shim-appended in-VM), so the whole refute block stands. PRESERVE: the belt describe `:977-1067` (10 tests), never-bypass `:940-950`, fail-closed workspace `:952-970`, all self-hosted tests, all 12 `cloud_policy_test.exs` tests. ADD: the genuinely-missing 2-tool-install CloudPolicy case (github+linear simultaneously — current file only has 1-tool+2-channel) via the SAME `insert_install/3` raw-SQL helper (never hand-roll new DDL, D54 drift risk); the 0/1/2-install argv table asserting in EVERY row that W12's full belt (`--tools ""` + `--disallowedTools` deny set + `--settings` deny JSON) is STILL PRESENT alongside the mcp flag.
- **D131 [DECIDED — merge contract re-derived; a 4th shape is a WATCH, not a free pass.]** Failing set at merge ⊆ {QueueTest `ready/1` dependency-plan hash; EnginePerfTest `recompute/1` complexity floor; RuntimeAdmissionTest owner-termination race} OR empty — the SAME trio as D124, re-confirmed across 13 completed runs (every failing run failed EXACTLY 1 test), RE-DERIVED fresh immediately pre-merge. NEW: `ChatLiveTest` session-persistence fired ONCE — on W12's own merge commit (which touched claude_chat.ex), with the very next run clean — n=1 flake signature, in EXACTLY the subsystem Slice B extends. It is NOT folded into the inherited set: any recurrence on a Slice-B run = STOP-AND-LOOK, never waved through. New tests 100% green; Format advisory-red inherited; Go slice rides its own gate + `make cli-build` refresh post-merge.
- **D132 [DECIDED — live serving is DOUBLY blocked beyond the human gate; both walls named, filed, never fabricated.]** (1) EGRESS: the production snapshot path locks the sandbox to `--allowed-domain api.anthropic.com` (deny-all-except, cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs:63/:374) — `api.githubcopilot.com`/`mcp.linear.app` are structurally unreachable; widening is a real D5/D24 host-safety tradeoff DECISION (backlog `connectors-cloud-egress-allowlist-widening`). (2) CREDENTIAL: D71's headersHelper is loopback-to-host (dead in-VM) and a second `--env` breaks the confinement pin — the sandbox-viable shape is a shim-mediated host-side header fetch embedded INTO the b64 config (backlog `connectors-cloud-credential-config-embed`; interacts with knob-4/D9, and github's MCP serves NOTHING credential-less, so this is load-bearing not polish). Both ride `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn` as W13 addendum observations. The debrief must say: :cloud is functional at the CONFIG layer; live tool serving remains gated + doubly blocked.
- **D133 [DECIDED — ledger honesty mapping.]** Go task `task-0c8b9214b7962152`: all 3 criteria closeable this wave at its own Go gate (criterion 3 lead-closes on merge). Sandbox task `connectors-cloud-sandbox-mcp-tools`: gains config-layer criteria (provable now); its live-init-frame criterion stays OPEN riding the human gate, its grant-scoped-token criterion stays OPEN riding knob-4/D9 (`task-5766dc5ca985ddc8`, D119). Crux `task-fe78ccb664308739`: NO criterion flips this wave (W13 evidence lands as notes; hygiene restamps = backlog `connectors-w13-ledger-hygiene`). ## Wave 13 — decisions (D126–D133, DECIDED with two verify rounds in hand; knob-3 wiring — the locked :cloud profile becomes FUNCTIONAL at the config layer)

Wave 13 closes knob-3's wiring half on top of W12's CloudPolicy policy half (D118): a Cloud workspace with
tool connectors installed launches a turn whose sandboxed claude is CONFIGURED with exactly those connector
MCP servers — and the W12 deny belt stays byte-intact. Every fork below closed with run output recorded in
`connectors-wave-13-2026-07-16`. The decisive verify corrections: `bp mcp serve` structurally CANNOT serve
connector tools (it bridges bp's OWN manifest nouns; github/linear are Catalog rows, not manifest nouns —
fixture grep = 0), and live in-sandbox serving is blocked by TWO walls beyond the human gate (egress lock +
no sandbox-viable credential). The wave's honest ceiling is the argv/config layer — exactly the wish's scope.

- **D126 [DECIDED — the mcpServers referent: per-provider HTTP descriptor entries, NEVER a stdio bp entry.]** The cloud mcpServers map = one `{"type":"http","url":<descriptor.url>}` entry per workspace tool-install (github→`api.githubcopilot.com/mcp/`, linear→`mcp.linear.app/mcp` — both toolDescriptors REAL, linear.ts:104/:132, github.ts:80/:199). URL stays single-sourced in Node: the Elixir HOST fetches descriptors via the existing `BridgeClient.fetch_tool_descriptors` loopback (host-side loopback works; fail-soft to `[]` — no bridge/secret ⇒ no entries, honest empty, a deploy precondition not a bug). REJECTED: an Elixir-static provider→url map (forks the URL out of its single Node owner, contra D75's no-endpoint law); a stdio `bp mcp serve` entry for connectors (bp binary ABSENT in-sandbox; the Go bridge walks `m.Commands` with zero connector concept — run-proven). `headersHelper` is STRIPPED from every cloud entry (it is a curl to the HOST loopback, dead across Firecracker — D71's precondition does not exist for :cloud); entries ship credential-less this wave.
- **D127 [DECIDED — flag ownership: the SHIM owns all mcp argv; 0 installs ⇒ byte-identical W12 argv.]** Elixir emits ONE new shim-own flag `--mcp-config-b64 <base64(mcpServers json)>` BEFORE the `--` separator (parseArgv breaks at `--`; anything after is handed to claude verbatim — probe-proven), and ONLY when ≥1 tool-direction install exists; with 0 installs the cloud argv is BYTE-IDENTICAL to W12 (no flag at all — the existing exact-argv pin survives as the 0-install row). The shim, when the flag is present: adds one `printf '%s' <b64> | base64 -d > /tmp/bp-cloud-mcp.json` line to `inSandboxScript` AFTER the frame line and BEFORE the exec, and appends `--mcp-config /tmp/bp-cloud-mcp.json --strict-mcp-config` to the claude command. D109's host-path finding SURVIVES (a host-written path still hard-fails; the config is materialized IN-VM); D109's "no mcp args" pin is AMENDED, not preserved. RUN-PROVEN at verify: this exact shape trips NONE of w12-shim-confinement-proof.sh's four assertions (4/4 PASS, exit 0) and keeps EXACTLY one `--env` pair — a second `--env` (e.g. a credential) WOULD break the pin; credentials never ride `--env`. `--strict-mcp-config` restricts SOURCES not COUNT (D69), so the 0/1/2 table is mechanically clean.
- **D128 [DECIDED — CloudPolicy stays the ONE :cloud posture owner; it gains the pure config-emission half.]** New pure function in `CloudPolicy` (e.g. `cloud_mcp_servers(installs, descriptors)`) composing `connector_tool_providers/1` (D118, shipped) with the host-fetched descriptors: keep only descriptors whose provider has a tool-direction install, emit `%{provider => %{"type"=>"http","url"=>url}}`, DROP headersHelper and anything malformed (fail-closed, reuse the `mcp_config/2` fold idiom incl. `Map.put_new` reserved-key guard). `claude_chat.ex` threads workspace + descriptors into `cloud_build_args/2` and base64-encodes — the policy module never fetches, the chat module never decides. Tests stay pure: descriptors are INJECTED in ExUnit (no live bridge needed); the bridge fetch is the same host-side idiom `setup_mcp` already uses.
- **D129 [DECIDED — Go `--tools` vocabulary: comma-separated NOUN list; bridge-only; shadow skip stays; parse stays syntactic.]** `--tools <noun,noun>` keyed on `Command.Noun` (the bridge's own naming key, mcp_bridge.go:130). A subset registers BRIDGE tools ONLY (never the curated task eight — `tasks` already means those); `bridgeShadowedIDs` skip STAYS active (inert for connector nouns, preserves never-double-expose; `--tools task` correctly yields zero). Parse-time validation is SYNTACTIC only: reserve `tasks`/`all`, reject empty comma tokens; noun EXISTENCE is NOT validated (the manifest loads after parse; an absent noun degrades to zero tools = the honest 0-install behavior, never a usage error). `parseMCPServeArgs` grows a subset return (small signature ripple confined to mcp_serve.go/_test.go). RUN-PROVEN at verify with a throwaway probe: `github,linear` → exactly 3 tools, `github` → 2, empty → 0, `task` → 0 (shadowed). HONESTY LAW: github/linear are NOT bp manifest nouns (fixture nouns grep = 0) — the flag is a GENERAL noun-subset filter proven with a SYNTHETIC manifest; it is NOT the transport by which cloud claude reaches connectors (that is D126's HTTP entries). Tests, docs, and the ledger must never claim otherwise.
- **D130 [DECIDED — the test amend/preserve map, surgical.]** AMEND `claude_chat_test.exs:897-924` (exact-argv pin) by SPLITTING into a 0-install row (byte-identical W12 argv — KEEP the exact-equality pin) and ≥1-install rows (new: `--mcp-config-b64` present pre-`--`, its decode == exactly the scoped map). PRESERVE `:926-938`'s host-path refutes VERBATIM for the ELIXIR-emitted argv — `--mcp-config`, `--strict-mcp-config`, and `/host/mcp.json` never appear in Elixir's args under D127 (both flags are shim-appended in-VM), so the whole refute block stands. PRESERVE: the belt describe `:977-1067` (10 tests), never-bypass `:940-950`, fail-closed workspace `:952-970`, all self-hosted tests, all 12 `cloud_policy_test.exs` tests. ADD: the genuinely-missing 2-tool-install CloudPolicy case (github+linear simultaneously — current file only has 1-tool+2-channel) via the SAME `insert_install/3` raw-SQL helper (never hand-roll new DDL, D54 drift risk); the 0/1/2-install argv table asserting in EVERY row that W12's full belt (`--tools ""` + `--disallowedTools` deny set + `--settings` deny JSON) is STILL PRESENT alongside the mcp flag.
- **D131 [DECIDED — merge contract re-derived; a 4th shape is a WATCH, not a free pass.]** Failing set at merge ⊆ {QueueTest `ready/1` dependency-plan hash; EnginePerfTest `recompute/1` complexity floor; RuntimeAdmissionTest owner-termination race} OR empty — the SAME trio as D124, re-confirmed across 13 completed runs (every failing run failed EXACTLY 1 test), RE-DERIVED fresh immediately pre-merge. NEW: `ChatLiveTest` session-persistence fired ONCE — on W12's own merge commit (which touched claude_chat.ex), with the very next run clean — n=1 flake signature, in EXACTLY the subsystem Slice B extends. It is NOT folded into the inherited set: any recurrence on a Slice-B run = STOP-AND-LOOK, never waved through. New tests 100% green; Format advisory-red inherited; Go slice rides its own gate + `make cli-build` refresh post-merge.
- **D132 [DECIDED — live serving is DOUBLY blocked beyond the human gate; both walls named, filed, never fabricated.]** (1) EGRESS: the production snapshot path locks the sandbox to `--allowed-domain api.anthropic.com` (deny-all-except, cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs:63/:374) — `api.githubcopilot.com`/`mcp.linear.app` are structurally unreachable; widening is a real D5/D24 host-safety tradeoff DECISION (backlog `connectors-cloud-egress-allowlist-widening`). (2) CREDENTIAL: D71's headersHelper is loopback-to-host (dead in-VM) and a second `--env` breaks the confinement pin — the sandbox-viable shape is a shim-mediated host-side header fetch embedded INTO the b64 config (backlog `connectors-cloud-credential-config-embed`; interacts with knob-4/D9, and github's MCP serves NOTHING credential-less, so this is load-bearing not polish). Both ride `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn` as W13 addendum observations. The debrief must say: :cloud is functional at the CONFIG layer; live tool serving remains gated + doubly blocked.
- **D133 [DECIDED — ledger honesty mapping.]** Go task `task-0c8b9214b7962152`: all 3 criteria closeable this wave at its own Go gate (criterion 3 lead-closes on merge). Sandbox task `connectors-cloud-sandbox-mcp-tools`: gains config-layer criteria (provable now); its live-init-frame criterion stays OPEN riding the human gate, its grant-scoped-token criterion stays OPEN riding knob-4/D9 (`task-5766dc5ca985ddc8`, D119). Crux `task-fe78ccb664308739`: NO criterion flips this wave (W13 evidence lands as notes; hygiene restamps = backlog `connectors-w13-ledger-hygiene`). The Go slice's "connector-scoped" label is aspirational vocabulary only (D129's honesty law).

## Wave 14 — decisions (D134–D143, DECIDED with run-proofs in hand; the Cloud Runner becomes a real multi-turn Barkpark Chat SESSION)

Wave 14 answers the user's direct steer — "Our Cloud Runner — it should be a chat session, utilizing the Barkpark Chat." W11–W13 made `:cloud` a single locked-down one-shot turn; today a second turn on a cloud session is SILENT AMNESIA (run-proven: the one-shot shim exits → the Session GenServer stops `:normal` → ChatLive respawns a context-free sandbox under "Send a message to resume it" copy). W14 gives the `:cloud` profile SESSION CONTINUITY: every turn stays a one-shot `claude -p` under CloudPolicy's full belt (D108 stands — no held subprocess, ever), and the memory rides the Barkpark Chat Session + a session-scoped persistent sandbox. Every fork below closed with run output recorded in `connectors-wave-14-2026-07-16`.

- **D134 [DECIDED — continuity mechanism: SESSION-SCOPED PERSISTENT SANDBOX (candidate 1) — create-once, stop-after-turn, auto-resume-on-next-turn.]** Run-proven live on the guerrilla (Pro) scope, vercel CLI 54.13.0: (a) a SECOND independent process can `vercel sandbox exec` a sandbox another process created — matches the per-turn shim-respawn model exactly; (b) /tmp persists between execs; (c) `vercel sandbox stop` auto-snapshots (Persistent=true default) and a later `exec` transparently auto-resumes with /tmp intact — /tmp is NOT tmpfs, it lives on the persisted xfs root (`/dev/vdb`), so the shim's pinned `HOME=/tmp/bp-cloud-home` (and the claude transcript under it) survives the stop/resume cycle. REJECTED: holding the sandbox RUNNING between turns (Provisioned Memory bills the whole wall-clock, idle or not); transcript export/import as primary (PROVEN viable — kept as the backlogged expiry fallback, `connectors-transcript-reseed-fallback`); context injection (the API path doesn't even persist the user's turn text today — assistant-only history, and it burns tokens + loses tool-call fidelity).
- **D135 [DECIDED — claude session identity: turn 1 `--session-id <uuid>`, turn N `--resume <uuid>` — run-proven non-interactive.]** `claude -p --session-id X` then `claude -p --resume X` carries context across one-shot `-p` invocations (codeword probe: turn 2 returned "ZEBRA-42", exit 0, session_id stable across turns). The transcript lives at `$HOME/.claude/projects/<cwd-slug>/<uuid>.jsonl` — lookup is cwd-slug-scoped with NO global-uuid fallback, and a missing transcript is LOUD (`No conversation found`, exit 1), never a silent fresh session. The shim's pinned HOME/CWD make the slug deterministic in every sandbox. The uuid is `session_opts[:session_id]` (the Barkpark session's public UUID — the same value `session_args/1` already emits on the self-hosted path). `cloud_build_args/2` appends the session flags into the post-`--` claude segment, so the shim passes them through untouched.
- **D136 [DECIDED — the sandbox binding lives ON chat_sessions: new nullable `cloud_sandbox_id` column.]** One sandbox per session is a 1:1 durable binding — a column inherits the `owner_workspace_id` tenant seal for free; the `chat_execution_leases` sibling-table shape (epoch/cursor/status) is fencing machinery this doesn't need. Written via a new `StudioChat.set_cloud_sandbox_id/2` that allows set AND clear — deliberately DISTINCT from write-once `set_provider_session_id/2` (the binding legitimately resets on sandbox expiry, D139). The transcript-blob boundary STAYS: Barkpark never stores or reads the CLI's .jsonl — under D134 that is the sandbox filesystem's job.
- **D137 [DECIDED — shim grows two additive pre-`--` flags + one sideband frame; teardown becomes mode-dependent.]** `--sandbox-id <id>`: skip `createSandbox()`, exec straight into the existing sandbox (it auto-resumes from its stop-snapshot). `--keep-sandbox`: normal-path teardown STOPS the sandbox (`vercel sandbox stop`) instead of REMOVING it; without the flag every current behavior is byte-preserved (unknown pre-`--` flags are already ignored — both flags are back-compat by construction; the W11/W12 proof scripts drive the shim flag-less and stay untouched). When the shim CREATES a sandbox in keep mode it emits ONE sideband NDJSON line on stdout BEFORE the claude stream: `{"type":"bp_sandbox","subtype":"created","sandbox_id":"<id>"}`. The Recorder pattern-matches `type:bp_sandbox`, persists the id (D136 setter), and SWALLOWS the frame — never broadcast to SSE/ChatLive, never appended as a message (the customer NDJSON stream stays clean, D112's spirit).
- **D138 [DECIDED — timeout + snapshot economics: stop-after-turn makes the 10m timeout PER-TURN, and think-time free.]** The sandbox's running-session timeout only counts while it runs; stopping after each turn means the existing 10m default bounds ONE turn, not the whole chat — no config-timeout dance, and the auto-hard-stop is the crash-safety net if the shim dies before stopping. Cost findings folded in: `vercel sandbox remove` does NOT delete stop-snapshots (~246MB each, 30d expiry) and stopped sandboxes are INVISIBLE to `sandbox ls` — keep-mode create passes snapshot-retention flags where available (`--keep-last-snapshots 1` / `--snapshot-expiration`), and the session-sandbox reaper is BACKLOGGED (`connectors-cloud-sandbox-reaper`), not this wave.
- **D139 [DECIDED — resume-ness derives from the BINDING; expiry = LOUD failure + honest reset, never silent amnesia.]** For `:cloud`, `--resume` is emitted IFF `session_opts[:cloud_sandbox_id]` is present (binding exists) — NEVER from `message_count` (the transport's `resume?` boolean stays inert for cloud; a message_count>0 session whose sandbox vanished must NOT `--resume` into an empty filesystem). If the bound sandbox is gone (expired/removed), the exec fails LOUDLY → the turn errors through the normal exit path → the binding is cleared → the NEXT turn mints a fresh sandbox and starts over with `--session-id` (documented, honest reset). The proven transcript re-seed (a byte-copied .jsonl resumes with full fidelity; provenance unvalidated — run-proof) is the backlogged amnesia-eliminator; it crosses the never-store-the-transcript boundary and needs its own decision record.
- **D140 [DECIDED — the /v1/chat API path persists the USER's turn text.]** Today only the Studio LiveView composer writes `role:"user"` rows (chat_live.ex:4380); the API path has ZERO `append_message` calls (traced) — a channel-driven session's persisted history is assistant-only, which no "real Barkpark Chat session" can accept. `create_message` appends the user row — ORDERING LAW: append AFTER `ensure_and_send` derives `resume?` from `message_count` (append-first would false-flip turn 1 into a resume). Profile-agnostic; existing tests that pre-seed user rows stay valid; a new test proves the ORGANIC user row end-to-end.
- **D141 [DECIDED — deny-belt invariant across turns; proof surface extends, never regresses.]** Every turn's argv carries the FULL W12/W13 posture (`--tools ""` + `--disallowedTools` + `--settings` deny JSON + single `--env ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` + `--mcp-config-b64` when installs exist); the new session flags are additive. Baseline re-proven green on origin/main this wave (targeted suite 141/0; w12 confinement proof 4/4 exit 0; `node --check` 0). The W13 exact-argv pins are AMENDED (like D127 amended D109) to carry the new session flags — 0-install byte-identity is now "byte-identical MODULO the session-identity flags", asserted explicitly. A NEW hermetic proof script (`w14-session-sandbox-proof.sh`, fake-vercel technique) proves the lifecycle black-box: `--sandbox-id` ⇒ no `create` invocation; `--keep-sandbox` ⇒ `stop` not `remove`; flag-less ⇒ byte-identical legacy behavior; bp_sandbox frame emitted on keep-mode create.
- **D142 [DECIDED — live multi-turn = NEW human gate; stub-key posture this wave.]** `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn` is SINGLE-turn scoped — its 2 criteria cannot honestly cover turn-2-carries-turn-1. W14 files `connectors-hg-live-cloud-multiturn` (live two-turn conversation, real key, guerrilla scope, codeword-recall assertion, zero orphans + snapshot cleanup). Residual risk named onto it: the exact fresh-HOME + env-API-key + resume combo was proven auth-orthogonally (resume is a pure file read before any API call) but never run end-to-end — the gate closes it for real. Never fabricated.
- **D143 [DECIDED — charter/git reconcile: the Wave-13 record is restored; local main is condemned as a base.]** The wish's "D1–D133 on main" was FALSE: origin/main's charter stopped at D125; the Wave-13 section (D126–D133) existed only in unpushed local commit 800fdb6a9 on a DIVERGED local main (5 unpushed docs commits across four epics vs 22 commits behind). This wave re-appends the Wave-13 block VERBATIM (pure-append diff-proven: 706a707,735) plus this Wave-14 section onto FRESH origin/main, committed on the epic's integration branch `loop-epic/connectors-w14-integration`. LAW: never branch from local main; the primary checkout was found sitting on a foreign loop-epic branch — worktrees off origin/main only.

## Wave 14 slice plan (5 slices; parent `task-e640bb01fca6ea38`; paper `connectors-wave-14-2026-07-16`; all opus; branch `loop-epic/connectors-w14-integration` off CURRENT origin/main — fd7dec6c9 or later; integration order W14-1/2/3 parallel → W14-4 after 1+2 → W14-5 independent)

- **W14-1 — `connectors-w14-cloud-session-argv` (p1, Elixir).** D135/D139/D141. `cloud_build_args/2` gains session identity: binding present ⇒ `--sandbox-id <id> --keep-sandbox` pre-`--` + `--resume <session_id>` post-`--`; binding absent ⇒ `--keep-sandbox` pre-`--` + `--session-id <session_id>` post-`--`. ## Wave 15 — decisions (D144–D151, DECIDED with two verify rounds in hand; the two-turn capstone RUNS — W14's A− closes to a proven mechanism)

- **D144 [DECIDED — the capstone hosts in ExUnit riding elixir.yml, NOT scripts/connectors/ — the lead-note placement is REJECTED on structural evidence.]** `connectors.yml`'s path filter is `connectors/**` + `scripts/connectors/**` + itself ONLY (lines 41-44) — it never fires on `api/**`; and the scripts layer is Node/shell that structurally cannot reach `--session-id`/`--resume` (computed in Elixir post-`--`, zero hits in cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs), the Recorder, or `chat_sessions.cloud_sandbox_id`. `elixir.yml` runs plain `mix test` on EVERY PR (no `paths:` key under `on:` — grep-proven zero matches) so the new file `api/test/barkpark_web/studio/claude_chat_cloud_session_test.exs` is auto-picked-up with zero workflow edits. "Blocking" stays discipline, not GitHub enforcement (D87: main has no branch protection — re-confirmed).
- **D145 [DECIDED — capstone construction law, RUN-PROVEN by a throwaway probe (2 tests / 0 failures) in a fresh worktree off origin/main 6eb0a0540.]** The probe drove the REAL `Recorder.ensure → Runtime.open → Port.open` pipeline twice on ONE session: counter file `["create","reuse"]` (exactly ONE create), turn-1 argv `--keep-sandbox` + `--session-id <uuid>` (no `--sandbox-id`), `cloud_sandbox_id == "sbx-777"` persisted, turn-2 argv `--sandbox-id sbx-777 --keep-sandbox` + `--resume <same uuid>` (no `--session-id`). LAWS the builder follows exactly: (1) `:sandbox_runner` config override, NEVER `:command` (bypasses `cloud_build_args` — vacuous-green trap); (2) two-behavior shim branching on `--sandbox-id` presence in its OWN argv — the create-count becomes a STRUCTURAL property of two real spawns, not a presence-grep (a broken always-create shim reads `["create","create"]` and fails); (3) the shim EXITS after its frames (NO trailing `cat`, unlike `bogus_key_shim`) — the Recorder must `{:stop,:normal}` so turn 2 spawns FRESH and re-reads the binding (loaded once at init, recorder.ex:295); frame order per turn: `bp_sandbox` → `result` → exit, one stdout; (4) between turns poll `Recorder.whereis(sid) == nil` (≤2s) before the second `ensure` — NEVER abrupt `Process.exit` kills (provokes a shared-mode `DBConnection.OwnershipError` test artifact); (5) `use Barkpark.DataCase, async: false` (shared sandbox mode is REQUIRED — the Recorder lives under a DynamicSupervisor and must reach the DB; data_case.ex:82) + `public_demo_studio: false` alongside `enabled: true` (else `enabled?/0` fail-closes). Postgrex `disconnected` [error] logs during the run are known drain noise, not failures. The task is RE-PERFECTED in place, not re-filed: 6 criteria (the direction's "7" was wrong), stub-kit line refs corrected (`argv_echo_binary` :1968→:2053, `bogus_key_shim` :1297→:1382, both +85; `put_chat_config` :69 exact), and all 4 BLOCKED notes are VOID — W14 merged as #3603 (4f00b84b7), the integration branch they cite is merged-and-deleted.
- **D146 [DECIDED — ExUnit scope honesty: `--env ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is NOT assertable from Elixir.]** That flag is shim-internal — emitted inside `cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs` `runTurn()` (:544-556) onto the real `vercel sandbox exec` argv, invisible to the Elixir→shim boundary the capstone drives. The capstone asserts the full Elixir-side deny belt on BOTH turns via the existing `assert_w12_belt_intact/1` (claude_chat_test.exs:1209); key confinement stays proven at the scripts layer (w12 proof, EXACTLY `[ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=sk-test]`). Never paraphrase the belt as including `--env`.

This proves the SHIM's own id round-trip; it is never sold as covering session identity, the Recorder, or persistence.

## Wave 16 — decisions (D152–D157, DECIDED with two verify rounds + run-proofs in hand; the single remaining buildable increment ships alone)

- **D152 [DECIDED — the failure signature is the COARSE ELIXIR-ONLY predicate: at-spawn binding present AND loud nonzero-INTEGER exit → clear; nothing else ever clears.]** Ground truth from an offline fake-`vercel` harness driving origin/main's shim (paper `connectors-wave-16-2026-07-16`): (a) a reuse-path exec failure propagates the vercel child's RAW nonzero code as the shim's own exit (proof: exec 47 → shim 47), UNMASKED by a failing finally-teardown (`teardownSandbox` discards the stop child's code, runner.mjs:318-321), with ZERO NDJSON frames on stdout and only benign `reusing/dispatching/teardown` log lines on stderr — vercel's own stderr provably never crosses the boundary (`run()` captures it into `res.err`, runner.mjs:214; `runTurn` returns only `res.code`, :562); (b) an in-VM claude/API failure exits the shim 0 (`vercel sandbox exec` does not propagate the in-VM code — proof: exec 0 + `result{is_error:true}` frame → shim 0), so a warm sandbox can NEVER be cleared by a mere API/auth error; (c) the strategic lean toward an emitter-owned SDK/not-found signal is IMPOSSIBLE as premised — the shim is CLI-only (D111), no vercel not-found text has ever been observed or fixture-pinned anywhere; a shim stderr-regex (REJECTED) would be a guessed fixture wearing a positive-signal costume, and a proactive `vercel sandbox get` pre-check (REJECTED) adds a per-turn round-trip on equally unobserved semantics, outside the task's own file scope. ACCEPTED COST, eyes open: a transient Vercel control-plane blip is byte-identical on the wire to a dead sandbox, so the signature clears a live warm binding on such a blip — one loud re-create, continuity honestly reset; the alternative (never clearing) permanently bricks the session with `--resume` into the void. The predicate keys on `is_integer(status) and status != 0`, NEVER a specific code — the create path exits 1 via main()'s catch while the reuse path propagates raw codes (proof: create-fail → 1, reuse-fail → 47); any fixed-integer match would misfire.
- **D153 [DECIDED — ONE BOUNDARY: the clear lives solely in the `:claude_chat_exit` handler (recorder.ex ~561; grep the clause text, not the line — D150's :561-569 has already drifted to :561-567).]** The loud dead-sandbox reuse reaches Elixir ONLY as a port `{:exit_status, integer}` → `{:claude_chat_exit, status, stderr_tail}` (claude_chat.ex:1319-1323). The wish's "claude_chat.ex ~883-887" is a MISLABEL: that switch is recorder.ex:883-887, Codex-only `%Event{}` machinery (its three kinds are emitted exclusively by runtime/codex/protocol.ex:82/123/139; zero matches in runtime/claude.ex) — it structurally never fires for a `:cloud` turn and stays untouched. `:crashed` (:DOWN handler, :576) and `:idle_reaped` (:589) are ATOM statuses broadcast from their own clauses and never re-enter :561; `is_integer` excludes them doubly. idle-reap / crash / clean-exit-0 all PRESERVE the binding (sandbox presumed alive; lazy-resume stays correct).
- **D154 [DECIDED — resume-attempt truth = the AT-SPAWN binding captured into Recorder state; NEVER a DB re-read at exit.]** `new_state/6` (recorder.ex:227-291) does not carry `cloud_sandbox_id` — init loads it (:146) only into `runtime_opts`, so the exit handler cannot today tell resume from create. The builder binds `load_cloud_sandbox_id(id)` ONCE in init and threads it into state as ONE new field (7th `new_state` arg). A DB re-read at exit is WRONG: a create turn's `bp_sandbox` capture (:522→:999) writes a fresh LIVE id mid-turn, and a re-read would wrongly orphan that healthy sandbox when the create turn dies loud; the at-spawn value (nil on a create turn) preserves it correctly. The clear is the unconditional existing primitive `StudioChat.set_cloud_sandbox_id(session_id, nil)` (studio_chat.ex:557, nil-clear unit-tested); a CAS-clear refinement is DECLINED — an extra read for a rare re-created-then-died-loud edge that is the same accepted D152 cost. The `is_binary(at-spawn id)` leg is also the `:cloud` gate for free: only the `:cloud` shim's bp_sandbox frame ever sets the column, so self-hosted sessions can never trip the clear.
- **D155 [DECIDED — the clear is SYNCHRONOUS INLINE in the handle_info body before `{:stop,:normal}`; the argv builder is untouched.]** The Ecto commit then strictly precedes the Registry `:via` name release, so the next turn's fresh Recorder (which re-reads the column at init, :146/:295) can never observe the stale binding; deferring to `terminate/2` or a spawned Task reintroduces the race — explicit build constraint. ZERO changes to `cloud_build_args`/`cloud_sandbox_args`/`cloud_session_args`: resume-ness already derives solely from the column (D139 law), so clearing it makes the next turn mint a fresh `--session-id` (no `--sandbox-id`, no `--resume`) and re-bind through the existing generic bp_sandbox capture.
- **D156 [DECIDED — proof discipline: third-turn honest-reset + create-fail-preserve counterfactual, extending the W15 capstone file, FAIL-FIRST pinned.]** RUN-PROVEN this wave in a fresh worktree off origin/main 978c7bd42: the capstone passes offline (2/0) via the symlinked-deps recipe (`ln -s <primary>/api/deps deps`; worktree-local `_build`, ~65s compile; deps-borrow works, `_build`-borrow stays broken), and a throwaway probe proved the gap LIVE — the binding SURVIVES a loud reuse failure today, and the protective assertion (`cloud_sandbox_id: nil`) REDS against origin/main (`right: %Session{cloud_sandbox_id: "sbx-stub-1"}`). The builder re-pins that red BEFORE wiring, then proves BOTH sides: (i) HONEST RESET — turn 1 creates+binds `sbx-stub-1`; turn 2 (stub sees `--sandbox-id`) emits ZERO frames and exits 1 → binding cleared inline; turn 3 argv is fresh-shape (`--session-id`, refute `--sandbox-id`/`--resume`) and re-binds a DIFFERENT id (`sbx-stub-2` — proving re-bind, not stale reuse), counter `["create","reuse-fail","create"]`; (ii) PRESERVE COUNTERFACTUAL — a CREATE turn that emits bp_sandbox then exits nonzero KEEPS the fresh binding (at-spawn nil → no clear). All D145 laws hold verbatim (`:sandbox_runner` config override never `:command`; shim exits after frames; poll `Recorder.whereis(sid) == nil` between turns; `DataCase async: false`; Postgrex drain noise is not failure). Probe-captured exit ground truth: `EXIT_STATUSES=[0, 1]` — create/success 0, vanished reuse 1.
- **D157 [DECIDED — wave shape: ONE slice, nothing padded; thereafter the Cloud-runner crux is FULLY human-gated.]** Secondary re-confirmed empty: both W15 ledger flags were reconciled honestly INSIDE W15 (`connectors-w13-ledger-hygiene`, done, PR #3632); the w12 seal is a deliberate substrate invariant (stamp.ex:201-208) and a raw `/v1/data/mutate` bypass is exactly the unsafe path the wish warned against — the recorded evidence stays as-is, remaining flips are lead actions. After this slice merges, the ONLY Cloud-runner work left is the two human gates `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn` + `connectors-hg-live-cloud-multiturn` (real ANTHROPIC key, guerrilla scope). Merge contract: inherited red set re-derived FRESH = {Format advisory, Sobelow advisory} ONLY — blocking Test / Prod compile / perf bench all GREEN at run 29496121685 (97b4fd2d5); the swatch-cell flake is GONE (fixed forward per D151, absent from current job lists — never cite it); re-check the freshest main run immediately pre-merge (978c7bd42's own run was still in_progress at Decide). ## Wave 17 — decisions (D158–D166, DECIDED with two verify rounds + run-proven RED pins in hand; the EPIC-PERFECTION breadth sweep — zero cloud-runner work, per the W16 seal)

- **D158 [DECIDED — wave shape: SEVEN slices, every one carrying a run-proven fail-first pin or green proof from Verify; nothing padded, one candidate dropped.]** The wish's ~3-6 stretches to 7 because Verify left ZERO unproven candidates: three RED-first bug pins actually executed against today's code (mint-count race `expected 2 to be 1`; cross-tenant state leak 3-failed subscription/cache/lock; two-installs collapse `map_size == 2` failed + LiveView `zzz-second-bot` absent from HTML), two test-gap slices fully mapped (plug mutation-kill proven: inverting require_chat_access.ex:58 reds exactly the nil-workspace case; tool-lifecycle branches confirmed 1:1), the docs edit drafted AND gate-proven (5596/5600B, both doc scripts PASS), and the npm fix run-proven in scratch (20→17, 496/496 vitest green). Dropping any of these discards verified work. Epic arithmetic reconciled: **41 open of 102 total children (53 done, 8 cancelled)** — the wish's "41 open" was exact; child_count=102 was never an open-count.
- **D159 [DECIDED — mint-race fix = reserve-first via `pg_advisory_xact_lock`, a NEW additive locked factory; the task's option (b) is REFUTED — no session-delete route exists.]** router.ex's `/v1/chat` scope (1659-1669) has NO DELETE verb, ChatController has no delete action, ChatClient has no deleteSession — and router.ex is wave-frozen, so lose-race-delete is unbuildable. Fix shape (spike-proven GREEN at Verify): checked-out pool client → `BEGIN` → `SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))` keyed `ws||':'||thread` (hashtext returns int4, auto-promotes to the bigint overload — no cast) → re-get inside the lock → mint+insert ONLY if missing → `COMMIT`. The `search_path=chat_bridge` pin SURVIVES the manual txn (it is a libpq connection startup param, pool.ts:63 — proven `["chat_bridge","chat_bridge"]` inside BEGIN). ADDITIVE: `createThreadSessionMap`/`createPgThreadSessionStore` and both interfaces stay byte-identical (3 test files' fakes survive); only index.ts:496's construction swaps to the locked factory. AC LANGUAGE CORRECTED: the ephemeral test DB holds only chat_bridge tables — "session count" is unprovable there; the pin is MINT-CALL COUNT (`SpyChatClient.createSessionCalls === 1` after a Promise.all race; the existing :341 race test does NOT catch this — it never asserts mint count). Accepted costs, eyes open: the mint HTTP round-trip runs while holding the lock + one pooled connection (first-message-only; a burst of >10 distinct new threads queues on pg.Pool max=10 — comment it); hashtext collisions serialize two unrelated threads at worst, never incorrectness. The verifier's `_spike-lock.test.ts` is throwaway — it must NOT ship.
- **D160 [DECIDED — state-key fix = per-install key-prefixing DECORATOR at mountInstall, prefix `${connector.id}:${install.installKey}`; NO data migration; NOT workspace-only.]** The bug is one layer below the task's name: `thread_session_map` is already composite-PK-safe; the hole is the Chat SDK's state-pg backend getting ONE global `keyPrefix="barkpark-bridge"` at boot (state-adapter.ts:28/39, built once at index.ts:494) — key_prefix is the ONLY tenant column in all 5 chat_state_* tables, so two tenants sharing a raw provider thread id (same human, two Telegram bots) collide on subscriptions/cache/locks (all three leaks RED-proven on real Postgres). Fix per the task brief's own design: `scopeStateAdapter(deps.state, connector.id, install.installKey)` at mountInstall's `Chat` construction (index.ts:294-298) — a generic decorator in state-adapter.ts that key-prefixes EVERY StateAdapter method, no core-loop change, startBridge:494 untouched. PREFIX SHAPE = connector.id + installKey, NOT workspace-only: connector_installs PK (provider, install_key) is globally unique, and Verify's Block D PROVED workspace-only still collides two installs of one provider in one workspace while connector+installKey isolates both — composing with W17-3. NO migration: the 5 tables hold ephemeral SDK coordination state; old global-prefix rows become unreachable dead rows, state reconstructs on the next inbound per thread. BUILDER LAW: wrap the FULL StateAdapter interface and inverse-map any method that RETURNS keys/thread ids; the shipped protective test must red on origin/main for the right reason (the unscoped shared adapter colliding) — Verify's Block A form is the proven pin. This slice is FABLE (interface-audit judgment + the one index.ts co-tenant, see D165).
- **D161 [DECIDED — catalog-multi-install fix = the LIST shape: `installs_by_provider` returns ALL installs per provider; one row per install, each with its own Disconnect keyed `(provider, install_key)`.]** The banner-with-links alternative is REJECTED — both options need the same underlying capability (surface all + resolve each for disconnect) and the list is the shape the ACs literally demand (BOTH visible, EVERY visible install disconnectable). RED-proven at both layers, entirely locally: catalog unit (two telegram installs → `map_size(by_provider) == 2` FAILS today) and LiveView (raw two-row INSERT + Stub bridge → second install's key absent from rendered HTML; the one visible Disconnect only ever removes the alphabetical winner — the second install's mounted adapter + labeled chat token are UNREVOCABLE from Studio). The "oldest wins" claim in catalog.ex:444-447's doc comment AND charter D104 was FALSE — install_key-alphabetical wins (`order_by [asc: provider, asc: install_key]` + Map.put_new first-seen); D104 corrected above, the builder corrects the doc comment. Happy path (one install per provider) stays byte-identical; existing connectors_live_test pins stay green.
- **D162 [DECIDED — capabilities-entry DROPPED from W17 (decline with evidence, stays open backlog); entry-only would MANUFACTURE a red required gate.]** The manifest→openapi coupling is mechanical (openapi.ex:44-48 builds from `Capabilities.manifest`) and the drift check is a REQUIRED step inside elixir.yml's Test job (elixir.yml:177-190, `git diff --exit-code docs/openapi.json`); local regen OOMs (D105, commit 4e8f10962); the only proven recipe is the CI-artifact capture that temporarily edits elixir.yml AND commits docs/openapi.json — BOTH wave-banned files. The task's own AC2 mandates the regen, so no narrowed shape exists. Declined on the task with a W17 note; delivery needs a lead-sanctioned exception wave for the temp-artifact recipe.
- **D163 [DECIDED — npm-audit ships the RUN-PROVEN non-breaking `npm audit fix`; residue documented under AC0's existing accepted-risk clause, never forced.]** Scratch-proven end-to-end: undici 6.24.1→6.27.0 + discord.js 14.26.5→14.27.0 + @discordjs/rest 2.6.1→2.6.2 (all in-range transitive bumps, package-lock.json ONLY, no @chat-adapter/* pins touched) closes the whole network-facing high cluster, 20→17; typecheck + format:check + 496/496 vitest green through the bump. Residue = accepted-risk with rationale in the PR: the vitest/vite chain (critical, DEV-ONLY, major-bump-only — no prod exposure) and chat-adapter-imessage's otel chain (npm's "fix" is a version-topology DOWNGRADE to 0.1.1; no safe forward path exists upstream). AC0's parenthetical already licenses exactly this — no AC rewrite needed.
- **D164 [DECIDED — auth.md is a NET-TRIM-PLUS-ADD to a 4-byte-headroom landing; the builder applies the PROVEN trim recipe, never re-derives under pressure.]** The addition (~900B: `chat` row, `public-read` row + two-mint-paths footnote, admin-superset Hierarchy paragraph — create_chat_token hardcodes ["chat"] because admin→:global→owner_workspace_id NULL reopens the tenant-less-session bug, chat_token_controller.ex:29-32) lands at 5596/5600B ONLY via the full recipe proven at Verify: (1) Roles-table alignment-padding strip (~314B, zero rendered content lost), (2) fold "### Why :ops is separate from :admin" into a parenthetical (~50B), (3) ~8 synonym-level prose tightenings (~180B). LAW: every individual trim is fact-checked against source — Verify caught a real near-miss where "(bearer or session)" nearly dropped from the Media footnote (RequireBearerOrSessionToken is a DISTINCT plug from bearer-only RequireToken; non-inferable fact, must survive). FLAG FOR THE FUTURE: docs/auth.md is now permanently at cap — every future edit is a full editorial pass, never a quick append.
- **D165 [DECIDED — the wave's ONE file-disjointness break: mint-race and state-key SHARE connectors/src/index.ts; ordering law: mint-race merges FIRST, state-key rebases.]** The blanket "all pairwise disjoint" claim from Survey was FALSE at file level for this pair — but a real 3-way `git merge` (ort) of both modeled diffs against the actual index.ts auto-merged clean in BOTH orders, byte-identical results: mint-race touches only the thread-session-map import + line ~496; state-key touches only the state-adapter import + mountInstall :294-298 (~200 lines apart, different import statements one line apart). Contingent on natural minimal diffs (extend the adjacent import statements, no import-block reformat; the decorator lives in state-adapter.ts, never thread-session-map.ts). Safety net at zero cost: both slices list index.ts in `files:` so the dispatch frontier sequences them; whichever reaches integration second (by law: state-key) rebases onto the first's merged commit before opening its PR. NO other slice touches index.ts (verified per-task at Verify).
- **D166 [DECIDED — W17 merge contract; gates re-derived FRESH, one flake already filed — cite, never re-file.]** Sobelow has MOVED out of elixir.yml into security.yml (advisory `continue-on-error`; the new blocking `mix-audit` job is GREEN). elixir.yml inherited reds = Format (advisory, non-connectors files) + ONE unfiled-looking flake that IS filed: ClaudeChatTest "removes the stderr capture file on close" (claude_chat_test.exs:747) is flake #3 of open task `elixir-flake-rotation-2026-07-16` (standalone, github #3558, OUTSIDE the epic tree — cross-referenced here by id because bp search won't surface it to epic-scoped readers). Main's tip run (29501348676) was in_progress at Verify — RE-POLL the freshest completed main run immediately pre-merge; failing set at merge must be ⊆ {Format advisory, Sobelow advisory, the filed flake} with the wave's own tests green. connectors.yml 10/10 green on main. ## Wave 18 — decisions (D167–D177, DECIDED with two verify rounds + run-proofs in hand; hardening sweep #2 — resurrect W17-5, then the offline-residue finish)

- **D167 [RATIFIED — PROCESS LAW: builder liveness = pushed-branch existence, NEVER claim heartbeats; a no-branch builder is FAILED — re-dispatch once or drop honestly.]** W17-5's builder claimed, wrote a live-looking now-line, then died with zero artifacts. Verify measured WHY re-dispatch is risky today: this host ran 12+ concurrent mix-test BEAM VMs at load 70–92 on 10 cores; a cold worktree compile (deps symlink shares SOURCE only — all 75 deps recompile) took ~23min, with multi-minute flat-CPU stretches that LOOK dead but are scheduler starvation. COROLLARY: a reviewer/dispatcher must NOT reap a quiet builder mid-compile — budget >30min for Elixir cold-compile slices; only branch/commit existence is truth. SECOND FAILURE MODE flagged: every worktree shares physical `barkpark_test` (MIX_TEST_PARTITION unset, pool 20/process, max_connections 100; 21 already in use at verify) — a `too many clients` FATAL means wait-and-retry, never redesign.
- **D168 [DECIDED — W17-5 re-dispatched VERBATIM; the environment itself is now RUN-PROVEN.]** The full builder recipe (fresh worktree off origin/main + `ln -s` deps + live Postgres + `CC=clang mix test`) executed GREEN end-to-end at Verify on the analog `optional_token_test.exs` ("4 tests, 0 failures"); `require_chat_access.ex` is byte-identical to d35b7cecc (zero drift, line-58 nil-guard intact); the claim is stale/reaped (epoch 4, worker null); no orphan branch/PR exists anywhere. The mutation-kill (inverting :58 reds exactly the fail-closed case) was already Verify-proven in W17. Task re-briefed with D167's contention warning; priority raised to 1.
- **D169 [DECIDED — whatsapp-window-persistence ships STORE-ONLY; store+wiring DECLINED this wave.]** Two verified reasons: (1) wiring touches index.ts in FOUR places (BridgeDeps 253-269, store construction 496, deps literal 531, handle()'s dispatch deps 320-336) — the SAME region as ack-first's onAck wrapper, breaking file-disjointness; (2) the window's READ side (`sendWhatsappWithinWindow`/`decideWhatsappWindow`) has ZERO production callers and the reply path never consults the window by design (whatsapp-window.ts:21-24) — wiring recordInbound would feed a table nothing reads. HONEST FRAMING LAW: the slice is persistence infrastructure (restart-survival of last-inbound, which the in-memory store provably cannot pass), NOT a reachable-defect fix. Premise corrections folded into the task: recordInbound is `(workspaceId, threadId, atMs)` (whatsapp-window.ts:125); whatsapp.ts has NO webhook handler body — the inbound spine is index.ts handle() → dispatchInbound. Wiring filed as `connectors-whatsapp-window-wiring` (needs a generic `Connector.inboundTimestampMs` hook — dispatch.ts is channel-agnostic by law). Harness proven: `thread-session-map.test.ts` ran 11/11 green here.
- **D170 [DECIDED — ack-first indicator = wire mountInstall's onAck wrapper to `adapter.startTyping(threadId).catch(() => {})`; src/index.ts ONLY; the honest harness is MANDATED.]** Defect worse than filed: `deps.onAck` is never set by any production caller — onAck is a TOTAL no-op on every channel; this is D21's first real user-visible effect. `startTyping` is REQUIRED on the vendor Adapter interface (messages-BSoJG691.d.ts:930) and implemented by all 6 channel adapters; the `.catch` is LOAD-BEARING (imessage THROWS in local mode). Telegram's DM typing is a vendor accident unrelated to onAck. VACUOUS-GREEN TRAP: spying dispatch.ts's or turn-loop's onAck greens WITHOUT touching index.ts — the test MUST call the real `mountInstall` export and spy `mounted.adapter.startTyping`; `slack-connector.test.ts:803-884` is a DECOY (inlined handle copy, stubbed runTurnImpl). Proven composition: whatsapp signed-webhook driver (whatsapp-connector.test.ts:373-420) + `createMockChat` (fixtures/chat-server.ts:39) + real `createChatClient`, so the real runTurn 202 fires onAck. Assert the CALL, not vendor acceptance. `turn-loop.test.ts` baseline ran 9/9 green. Tool-direction connectors (github/linear) never enter mountInstall — naturally out of scope. This slice is FABLE.
- **D171 [DECIDED — oauth-config-env-plumbing = runtime.exs-ONLY; the offline Config.Reader harness is the proven template.]** `catalog.ex:305-314/360-371` reads `:slack_client_id`/`:linear_client_id`/redirect_uris from the `Barkpark.Connectors` app config, but runtime.exs (132-141) NEVER writes those keys — the cards can never leave the not-configured gate. Template `runtime_task_lease_ttl_test.exs` ran 5/5 green here (Config.Reader.read! prod + merge + put_env/on_exit). Names are NOT secret-shaped — zero scrubbed-env interaction. Red-first: the new test reds on unmodified runtime.exs.

## Wave 19 — decisions (D178–D186, DECIDED with two verify rounds + run-proofs in hand; hardening sweep #3 — the buildable-offline vein is mined out after this wave)

- **D178 [DECIDED — whatsapp-window-wiring ships WRITE-ONLY, honestly reframed; the hook fires INSIDE dispatchInbound keyed on the RESOLVED workspaceId.]** The read side CANNOT be wired non-vacuously: the ONLY outbound post in the package is reply-to-inbound (index.ts:328-331/362/367/371) — a `decideWhatsappWindow` consult milliseconds after the window opened is mechanically always-true, and no proactive/business-initiated send API exists anywhere (grep-proven). The slice is persistence-infrastructure completion ("replies were never at risk"), never a reachable-defect fix. WORKSPACE-KEY RULING (settles the surveyor contradiction): the write fires inside `dispatchInbound` AFTER workspace resolution (dispatch.ts:194-196), keyed on the RESOLVED `workspaceId` — for credential-bound connectors resolved==install.workspaceId is PROVEN (resolveWorkspace and lookupInstall read the same `connector_installs` row by (provider, install_key)), but payload-team-id connectors (slack, teams) CAN diverge, and the resolved id already keys the Session (:221) and the credential (:200); a handle()-side `install.workspaceId` key would bake a credential-bound assumption into a channel-agnostic seam. Shape (D169's 4 touch points confirmed, +1..+8 line drift): optional `Connector.inboundTimestampMs?(payload): number|null` (types.ts:463 region; whatsapp.ts sets it to the orphaned, already-unit-tested `whatsappInboundTimestampMs` :206-229 — pure wiring, no new parsing) + optional `DispatchDeps.recordInbound?(workspaceId, threadId, atMs)` (mirrors reply/onAck); index.ts constructs `createPgWhatsappWindowStore(pool)` and threads it through. The write lands BEFORE the credential/session/turn steps — Meta's window opened the instant the user spoke, independent of whether OUR turn later drops or throws. NULL-TIMESTAMP POLICY: `Date.now()` fallback, never skip-the-write (skip reads as window-CLOSED and defeats the store; now() is monotonically safe under the GREATEST upsert); the Meta-redelivery caveat rides as a code comment. Red-first test = composition of the two harnesses proven 8/8 green at Verify on real local Postgres (ack-first's real `mountInstall` + HMAC-signed Meta webhook whose body carries timestamp "1752451200" → 1752451200000ms; whatsapp-window-pg's pool-restart proof): fire the signed webhook, assert `getLastInboundAt("ws-alpha", thread) === 1752451200000` through a FRESH pool — RED before wiring (null), GREEN after.
- **D179 [DECIDED — oauth-return poll + slack-token-relabel FOLD into ONE slice, ONE builder; D98 is FILE-level law on connectors_live.ex — textually-disjoint regions do not exempt.]** Poll (D175's poll-variant, re-verified: zero handle_info/send_after/PubSub in the file today; router.ex stays walled): reuse the directory's proven idiom (studio_live.ex `:access_expiry_tick` ~107-120; chat_live.ex `:turn_tick` ~1814-1822) — schedule only while an oauth link is staged and that provider's installs are empty, tick calls `reload/1` (:401-405, unchanged), stop once the install appears; tenancy holds by construction (`installs_by_provider` is socket-workspace-scoped, fail-closed inner join — a poll has no topic to leak). Deterministic red-first test = `send(view.pid, :oauth_poll_tick)` (exact idiom: studio_live_read_liveness_test.exs:137/189/234) after a raw-SQL install insert against the SAME mounted view — proves the flip without re-navigation. Relabel (#3239 FOLDED IN, verified pure-Elixir end-to-end: the Node OAuth callback writes NO label — grep api_tokens/label over connectors/src = zero hits; `label` lives only on api_tokens (api_token.ex:17), set once at mint (auth.ex:326); NO update-label primitive exists): new Auth relabel function (plain changeset update on the existing column — no uniqueness constraint on :label, no migration) + relabel-on-reload reconciliation: when a Slack install exists and its workspace still holds a live `connector:slack:oauth` token, relabel it to `connector:slack:<install_key>` idempotently — closing the limitation filed at connectors_live.ex:337-341 and collapsing Slack's two-label disconnect asymmetry. Red-first: seed install row + oauth-labelled token, reload, assert the label — fails today; the pre-callback pin (connectors_live_test.exs:687-690) stays untouched and green. FALLBACK if the folded diff exceeds one honest slice: hard SEQUENCE (poll merges first, relabel rebases on top) — NEVER two parallel branches on connectors_live.ex. D104 token law binds: no new ad-hoc CSS class for any refresh indicator.
- **D180 [DECIDED — cloud-claude-version-pin executes the banked D176 recipe VERBATIM; every anchor re-verified byte-for-byte at survey.]** runner.mjs:79 `CLAUDE_INSTALL` unversioned today; :540/:541 is the snapshot+fallback convergence (assert at the END of createSandbox covers BOTH provisioning paths); cloud_policy.ex:57 names 2.1.211 and explicitly defers the assertion to this task; the pin file is absent (nothing stole the recipe since W12). NEW `scripts/connectors/cloud-claude-pinned-version.txt` seeded 2.1.211 — NEVER shared with `scripts/claude-pinned-version.txt` (2.1.206, self-hosted host-CLI pin, different surface/value/proof-lane). Proof = fake-vercel hermetic harness extension (w12 exercises the fallback path, w14's `CLOUD_SANDBOX_SNAPSHOT` exercises the snapshot path; the NUL-delimited argv-capture idiom is reused, not reinvented): fake vercel returns a WRONG version → RED; the pinned version → GREEN; a mismatch surfaces LOUDLY, never silently.

The 5 HUMAN GATES (hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn, hg-live-cloud-multiturn, linear-live-oauth-gate, slack-handshake-live-verify, p4-live-telegram), the DESIGN-GATED cloud items (run-secrets D9 `task-5766dc5ca985ddc8`, cloud-credential-config-embed, per-workspace-execution-profile, cloud-egress-allowlist-widening, warm-pool D22 `task-4780b75b68962056`), D162's capabilities-entry, discord slash (its blocker `connectors-discord-dual-transport-design` IS a decision, not offline code), `connectors-cloud-shim-sdk-auth` (needs a real platform token), `connectors-cloud-interactive-multiturn` (no stdin/control channel into a one-shot sandbox exists), `connectors-transcript-reseed-fallback` (its own AC demands amending the ratified never-store-the-transcript boundary first) — ALL stand. run-secrets D9 is noted as the ONE technically-offline-buildable peripheral, declined on the wish's own exclusion + caller-lessness (its sole consumer is itself design-gated). `task-6c358e166b6c7eaf` (P2 rollup) stays honestly open at 3/4 — its last AC IS the live-Telegram human gate, not unstamped shipped work; never evidence-close it. #3238 was already cancelled at W18 (D177) — dropped from the wish list as stale.
