## Wave 27 — decisions (D225–D231, DECIDED from 5 verify assignments ALL carrying run output incl. an EXECUTED two-site red probe + a full offline vendor-seam roundtrip; the DUAL-TRANSPORT DESIGN+BUILD wave: the W18/D175 decline gets its ratified design AND its build — Discord keeps its Gateway AND gains slash commands; paper `connectors-wave-27-2026-07-18`)

- **D225 [DECIDED — TRANSPORTS ARE ADDITIVE, DERIVED FROM CONNECTOR SHAPE. No declared transport set, no registry field, no schema, no UI — a connector's transports are exactly the optional members it carries (`webhook?`, `listen?`), checked INDEPENDENTLY.]** `isLeaseManaged` (connectors/src/index.ts:245-251) DROPS its `connector.webhook === undefined` clause — the predicate becomes "channel/both direction AND `typeof listen === 'function'`" (has a leased transport), full stop. `bringUp` (index.ts:597-630) stops being a strict if/else-if ("three transports, three fates") and becomes two INDEPENDENT checks: `if (connector.webhook)` → `mounts.mount(...)` into the W8 replica-safe webhook index; separately `if (isLeaseManaged(connector))` → leave `listen()` for the lease coordinator's onAcquire, `else if (typeof connector.listen === 'function')` → direct listen (the existing fallback, preserved). Telegram/iMessage (listen-only) and Slack/Teams/WhatsApp (webhook-only) each still take exactly one branch — byte-identical behavior, run-proven: no test fixture anywhere in connectors/test combines webhook+listen, and the full sibling census (only install-lease.test.ts + mount-reconcile.test.ts reference these predicates at all) shows ZERO sibling reds from the redefinition. FAILURE MODE KILLED (run-proven RED on origin/main, probe assertion `expected false to be true` at isLeaseManaged(dual)): adding a webhook block flips isLeaseManaged false → the coordinator's filter excludes the install → onAcquire never fires → the Gateway silently dies while slash commands work — normal DMs/mentions stop with no error anywhere. The CONTROL proved the red is for the right reason: the same fixture minus `webhook` IS lease-managed today. COSMETIC-BUT-MANDATORY same-diff fix: bringUp's mount log ternary (index.ts:620-628) is mutually exclusive by construction and would print only "(webhook)" for a dual install — rewrite it to join both labels in S1's same edit, or the first dual install ships a misleading operator-facing log.
- **D226 [DECIDED — THE XOR LIVES AT TWO SITES; BOTH EDITED IN LOCKSTEP. Fixing index.ts alone is a NO-OP for the coordinator that actually claims leases.]** `socketPollConnectors()` (connectors/src/tenant/install-lease.ts:274-280) hand-copies the identical `connector.webhook === undefined && typeof connector.listen === 'function'` predicate — it CANNOT import isLeaseManaged (index.ts imports install-lease.ts; importing back is circular). Run-proven RED at this second site independently: with a dual fixture registered, `reconcileOnce()` over real Postgres returns `summary.acquired === 0`, onAcquire never fires, no lease row (probe assertion `expected +0 to be 1`) — the exact failure class the wave exists to kill, one file over. THE FIX: drop the `webhook === undefined` clause at BOTH sites in the same diff, with cross-reference comments naming each other (the predicate is duplicated by structural necessity — the comments make the lockstep contract explicit for the next editor). S1's real test must also assert `summary.failures === 0` alongside `acquired === 1` (a DB error would also surface as acquired=0 — airtight the red's cause). ZERO change needed at the other census'd sites, verified exhaustively: mount-reconcile.ts:145's `webhookConnectors()` is presence-only (`webhook !== undefined`) and already additive; mounts.ts is idempotent per-(provider,installKey) — ONE mount() call for Discord's ONE Chat regardless of transport count; takeDown/shutdown (index.ts:689,704) call the EXPORTED predicate and pick up the redefinition automatically; webhook-server.ts's only presence check (:802-806) is an additive guard; gateway.ts's `webhookOptions` is a vendor SDK parameter name — a confirmed grep false-positive, do not chase it.
- **D227 [DECIDED — THE LEASE STAYS PER-INSTALL. Zero schema change; dual transport has NO double-mount or starvation surface.]** `connector_install_leases` PK is `(provider, install_key)` with no transport column (schema.ts) — nothing to migrate. Only the SOCKET needs single-replica ownership (D93's law); webhooks are stateless and every replica serves them via the W8 mount index, so a per-(install,transport) lease would model a problem that does not exist. onAcquireTransport/onReleaseTransport (index.ts:789-820) touch ONLY `listen`/`stopListening` — zero shared mutable state between the two transports of one install: acquiring/releasing the Gateway lease cannot affect the webhook mount and vice versa. D93/D94/D95's replica-safety scope statements survive verbatim; D94's "exact complement of mount-reconcile's filter" prose becomes STALE wording once D225 lands (the sets now overlap on Discord) — the install-lease.ts comments at :67 and :270 get rewritten in S1's same diff to state the new relationship (webhook-mount set and leased-listen set are independent, overlapping on dual-transport connectors).
- **D228 [DECIDED — SLASH COMMANDS RIDE THE VENDOR SEAM; the strategize-round "hand-roll node:crypto Ed25519" leaning is RETIRED by run-proof.]** `chat.webhooks['discord']` resolves to the vendor `DiscordAdapter.handleWebhook` (chat/dist/index.js:2287 builds the closure → Chat.handleWebhook → ensureInitialized → adapter.handleWebhook) — the one genuine surveyor conflict is SETTLED against the "bespoke seam extension needed" claim by a full offline roundtrip: signed PING → 200 `{type:1}`; forged/unsigned interaction → 401 "Invalid signature"; signed APPLICATION_COMMAND → synchronous 200 `{type:5}` (deferred ack, returned BEFORE dispatch resolves) with dispatch riding `waitUntil` (BG_TASKS_REGISTERED 1). The vendor ships the ENTIRE interaction protocol (raw-body Ed25519 via discord-interactions' verifyKey/WebCrypto — already installed transitively, ZERO new dependency, do NOT add a direct import; PING→PONG; type-5 deferred ack; waitUntil-backgrounded dispatch into `chat.processSlashCommand`). S2 therefore has the SMALL shape: discord.ts declares `webhook: { keySource: "path" }` (per-install URL `/connectors/webhooks/discord/:installKey` — the WhatsApp path-keyed precedent, D39's seam verbatim; per-install `publicKey` banked since D41 for exactly this day) and the existing generic demux (webhook-server.ts → lookupInstall → mounts.handlerFor → chat.webhooks.discord) serves it with ZERO changes to webhook-server.ts/mounts.ts/types.ts logic. Rule 1 stands as law: drive `chat.webhooks[provider]`, NEVER `adapter.handleWebhook` directly (chat auto-initializes via ensureInitialized on first call — run-proven; a direct adapter call on an un-init'd adapter 500s).
- **D229 [DECIDED — THE ACTUALLY-BROKEN GAP IS HANDLER REGISTRATION, and the reply path is vendor-routed via AsyncLocalStorage. Four vendor-contract laws, each preventing a SILENT failure.]** connectors/src registers ZERO `chat.onSlashCommand` today (grep: no hits) — slash commands ALREADY arrive over the Gateway (no webhookUrl passed → setupLegacyGatewayHandlers registers InteractionCreate → deferReply → processSlashCommand), get acked, and drop into an EMPTY handler array. S2's primary red-first slice is registering `chat.onSlashCommand` in mountInstall, funneling into the SAME dispatch/turn loop the message handlers use: `handle(event.channel.id, event.text, (out) => event.channel.post(out), event.raw)` — the SlashCommandEvent carries `channel` (NO thread), and the reply closure stays channel.post-shaped because the vendor adapter routes it: `handleApplicationCommandInteraction` wraps dispatch in `requestContext.run({slashCommand:{channelId, interactionToken, ...}})` (AsyncLocalStorage) and `postMessage` first tries `tryPostSlashResponse` → first reply `PATCH /webhooks/{applicationId}/{interactionToken}/messages/@original`, follow-ups `POST ...?wait=true` — run-proven for BOTH a webhook-shaped and a Gateway-shaped interaction, each using its OWN token, ONE registered handler serving both. THE FOUR LAWS: (a) the WebhookOptions key is `waitUntil` NOT `backgroundTask` — the JSDoc at chat/dist/index.js:2208 is STALE; passing the wrong key still acks but dispatch is NEVER backgrounded (silent drop). (b) The reply MUST target `event.channel.id`: tryPostSlashResponse's guard (`slashContext.channelId !== threadId` → undefined) silently falls back to a normal channel POST — the reply drops off the interaction with no error. (c) Dispatch stays INSIDE the vendor's processSlashCommand/requestContext.run — deferring the handler onto a fresh macrotask outside run() loses the ALS store (silent fallback, same as (b)). (d) Offline tests MUST stub `globalThis.fetch`: the interaction follow-up hardcodes `DISCORD_API_BASE` + global fetch — neither `this.apiBaseUrl` nor the connector's `options.fetch` seam reaches it; a test trying options.fetch silently hits real discord.com.
- **D230 [DECIDED — DISCORD'S EITHER/OR, WORDED PRECISELY. "Dual transport" = the socket stays up for MESSAGES while INTERACTIONS move wholly to the webhook once the endpoint URL is set — NEVER both firing for one event.]** Discord's docs settle contradiction (c): interaction delivery is MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE per application — Gateway INTERACTION_CREATE XOR the portal Interactions Endpoint URL; setting the URL stops Gateway interaction delivery app-wide but does NOT affect MESSAGE_CREATE (DMs/mentions keep flowing on the same socket). The unqualified claim "both transports run concurrently for interactions" is platform-contradicted and MUST NOT enter any doc. Architecturally moot for the bridge: the SAME registered handler (D229) serves whichever transport delivers — register now, the HTTP path is purely additive. ACK SEMANTICS: the right 3s-ack precedent is webhook-server rule 2 (sync response + mandatory waitUntil, Slack-proven) NOT D21's onAck/startTyping (a Gateway typing side-channel that fires too late and cannot BE the HTTP response); the vendor returns type-5 synchronously by construction; interaction tokens live 15 minutes — the async turn has a hard 15-min delivery ceiling, not just the 3s ack. OPS RUNBOOK LAWS (documented ops steps, never fake-proven code): (1) SEQUENCING IS DISCORD-ENFORCED — the portal PING-validates the endpoint at save time (signed PING must get a PONG or the save is REFUSED), so the webhook route must be deployed and live BEFORE the URL is saved; (2) NAMED FAILURE MODE — Discord runs ongoing invalid-signature probes and SILENTLY REMOVES a failing URL (email/System DM only): a future Ed25519-path regression manifests as slash commands "reverting to unconfigured" with zero bridge error; (3) command REGISTRATION is `PUT /applications/{id}/commands` — guild-scoped (`/guilds/{guild.id}/commands`) updates instantly for dev, global takes ~1h — not offline-stubbable, stays an ops step in `connectors/docs/discord-byo-bot.md`.

Pure-Node wave on connectors.yml; D-numbers START AT D225 (the wish's "D222+" premise was STALE — D222-D224 consumed by W26/LiveAuth #4128); two slices SEQUENCED because both orbit index.ts.]** Baselines run-proven green on origin/main: install-lease + mount-reconcile 16/16, discord-connector + webhook-server 54/54 (real Postgres, CONNECTORS_REQUIRE_DB=1), prettier clean (BLOCKING whole-package — run `npx prettier --write` on every new file before push, W18/W22 law), no open connectors PRs, both driving tasks unclaimed. ## Wave 28 — decisions (D232–D236, DECIDED from 4 verify assignments ALL carrying run output incl. the FULL red-first harness executed 5/5 green under the repo's actual vitest; the FINISH wave: W27-2 builds as the whole of W28 — Discord slash commands wire into the turn loop; paper `connectors-wave-28-2026-07-18`)

- **D232 [DECIDED — COMMAND-PREFIX LAW: the funnel text is `` `${event.command} ${event.text}`.trim() ``, NEVER `event.text` alone. This AMENDS D229's verbatim funnel call — the only design change this wave makes, forced by two run-proofs meeting in the middle.]** The vendor's `SlashCommandEvent.text` carries ONLY option VALUES (run-proven: "/ask hi" → `command:"/ask"`, `text:"hi"`; a bare no-option command → `text:""` — chat/dist/messages-BSoJG691.d.ts:2265 documents it), and `dispatchInbound` drops any empty-trimmed text at the TOP, before tenant resolution or Session work (`core/dispatch.ts:192-193 → {status:"dropped_empty_input"}`, existing test green). D229's charter-verbatim `handle(event.channel.id, event.text, ...)` would therefore make every argument-less command — the SINGLE most common shape, a bare `/status` or `/help` — silently no-op: type-5 ack shown, dispatch dropped, no reply ever posted, Discord surfaces "The application did not respond" after the 15-minute token TTL. The fix is ONE line inside the NEW `onSlashCommand` registration in index.ts (`event.command` is documented always-present/non-empty, so the joined string can never be empty); it lives OUTSIDE core/ — the zero-core-loop-diff law holds by construction. Explicitly BLESSED side effect: the LLM turn now sees the invoked command verb (e.g. "/help topic search" arrives byte-identical to what the user typed) — semantically desirable, and grep-proven collision-free (no `startsWith("/")`/command parsing anywhere in core/, turn/, tenant/, connector/). The red-first suite MUST include a bare no-option command case proving dispatch receives non-empty text — the case that reds under D229-verbatim wiring.
- **D233 [DECIDED — ADOPT THE PROVEN HARNESS VERBATIM. The preflight probe ran 5/5 GREEN under the repo's actual vitest (globals:false, environment:node), prettier-clean, WHOLE-PACKAGE tsc-clean — the builder starts from it, renamed to `connectors/test/discord-slash-commands.test.ts`, and does not re-derive.]** The full skeleton is banked in paper `connectors-wave-28-2026-07-18` (verification section) + `scratchpad/w28-preflight-probe.PROVEN.test.ts` (volatile). ## Wave 29 — decisions (D237–D242, DECIDED from 6 verify assignments ALL carrying run output incl. an EXECUTED old-shim inertness probe, a repeated-flag CLI parse probe, and vendored-source payload tracing; the EGRESS-WIDENING DESIGN+BUILD wave: D132 wall 1 gets its ratified design AND its offline-provable build — per-connector `--allowed-domain` widening of the W12 deny-all sandbox; paper `connectors-wave-29-2026-07-18`)

- **D237 — Egress hosts are a property of the connector TYPE, derived from the MCP descriptor URL — no new registry field.** The Elixir Catalog declares NO host field anywhere (verified: `@tool_providers` entries carry id/name/blurb/connect_mode/direction/credential_label/help_url/gate only); the ONLY host material in the whole pipeline is `toolDescriptor.url` (github.ts:80 → `api.githubcopilot.com`, linear.ts:97 → `mcp.linear.app`) flowing through the bridge's tool-descriptors route into `CloudPolicy.cloud_mcp_servers/2` — the standing installed∩descriptors fold (D126/D128). The MCP host ALONE suffices, code-proven: the W25 credential path is 100% host-side (D213 REJECTED in-VM fetch; `refreshCredential`'s single call site is the bridge's tool-headers route; `cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs` imports zero `connectors/src` code and reaches the bridge only over loopback). Therefore `api.linear.app` (OAuth token endpoint, bridge-side) and `api.github.com` (PAT validation, bridge-side) must NEVER appear in the sandbox allowlist — the wish's "api.linear.app" phrasing was WRONG as a sandbox host. No optional declared-extra-hosts registry field this wave (zero consumers today; additive later if a future connector's MCP server and client-reached API host ever differ). Failure modes prevented: a hand-maintained host list drifting from the real descriptor URL; per-workspace host config (hosts are type properties, not install properties); bridge/OAuth hosts leaking into the VM's egress.
- **D238 — D126/D128 split holds: Elixir derives + emits repeated `--egress-host <host>` pre-`--`; the dumb shim unions with its anthropic base and expands to repeated `--allowed-domain` at create.** `cloud_build_args/2` gains a `cloud_egress_args` sibling fold keyed on the SAME `CloudPolicy.cloud_mcp_servers/2` result the mcp-config fold already uses, emitting one `--egress-host <host>` pair per unique sanitized host, placed AFTER `cloud_sandbox_args` and before `--`; 0 surviving servers → `[]` → the 0-install argv is byte-identical (the exact-array assertions at claude_chat_test.exs:918/:1293 stay green UNTOUCHED — that is the zero-regression proof anchor). The shim's `parseArgv` collects `--egress-host` values; `createSandbox` unions them with the env `ALLOWED_DOMAIN` base (`api.anthropic.com`, runner:85), dedupes, and expands the SNAPSHOT-path `isolation` array (runner:719-721) to repeated `--allowed-domain` flags. ELIXIR NEVER EMITS `api.anthropic.com` — the anthropic base stays the shim's env-configurable property; that invariant is what preserves deny-all-plus-anthropic exactly when no tool is installed. Repeated flags, NOT a b64 list: hosts are constrained DNS tokens (D239), so D214's b64 rationale (arbitrary-JSON quoting hazard) does not apply; N flags in → N flags out is the legible model and the shim branch stays ~3 lines. ROLLOUT PROVEN INDEPENDENT (executed probes): an OLD shim logs-and-ignores each unknown pre-`--` token WITHOUT consuming a value (parseArgv:240-243) and ran a FULL turn to completion (exit 0) with `--egress-host api.githubcopilot.com` present — so the halves land/deploy in EITHER order: Elixir-first degrades to today's fail-closed posture (MCP host unreachable, no crash, no unintended widening); shim-first is a byte-identical no-op. NO co-ride mandate; two file-disjoint parallel slices.
- **D239 — Host sanitizer: new pure function, SafeOutbound's SHAPE half only, fail-closed.** No host sanitizer exists anywhere in the repo (`cloud_tool_server_entry/2` checks only `is_binary and != ""`). The derivation applies per descriptor url: `URI.new/1` strict parse → require scheme `https` → require non-blank `.host` → REJECT any userinfo (refuse the whole descriptor, SafeOutbound's posture — never silently strip) → `String.downcase` → strip a single trailing dot → require ASCII DNS-name shape (`^[a-z0-9.-]+$`, reject IP literals v4/v6) → REJECT a non-default port (a declared MCP host never needs one; dropping it silently would widen the host on all ports). Any rejection → that connector contributes NO host — fail-closed, never a crash, never a partial. NEVER the DNS half of SafeOutbound: the offline proof must stay network-free; SSRF-style resolution defense is the wrong tool for canonicalizing static declared strings. Failure modes prevented: case/trailing-dot mismatch against Vercel's SNI matcher; userinfo/IP-literal smuggling via the bridge-fetched ("host-fetched, INJECTED") descriptor.
- **D240 — Proof form: SET-EQUALITY, never containment, at BOTH layers, red-first.** Elixir: ExUnit in cloud_policy_test.exs (reusing its existing github/linear descriptor fixtures) + claude_chat_test.exs asserting the FULL egress segment — github install → exactly `["--egress-host","api.githubcopilot.com"]`; github+linear → exactly the two-host set (sorted, deduped); 0 installs → `[]`; malformed/userinfo/http/IP/ported descriptor → dropped. Blast radius pinned AT DECIDE (re-run green, 167 tests/0 failures across the 4 argv-decoding files): tests WITH installs hard-code the pre-`--` array (`Enum.take(args, sep)` at claude_chat_test.exs:958-964) — same-PR updates required; `claude_chat_per_workspace_profile_test.exs` (the real W23-1 test) and `claude_chat_cloud_session_test.exs` are IN the gate sweep. Shim: a FOURTH sibling `scripts/connectors/w29-egress-allowlist-proof.sh` (w12/w14/w25 fake-vercel idiom: NUL-delimited argv captures, locate the create by content not file order, `trap` teardown) + a fourth BLOCKING step in connectors.yml's shim-confinement job, asserting the multiset of `--allowed-domain` values on the `sandbox create` capture: with `--egress-host api.githubcopilot.com` → exactly {api.anthropic.com, api.githubcopilot.com}; with none → exactly {api.anthropic.com} (byte-preserved baseline); base repeated in egress input → deduped (no doubled flag). Verified at Decide: NO existing proof asserts an exact/closed set on create argv (w12 never reads it; w14/w25 use containment only) — adding flags breaks nothing, and w12/w14/w25 MUST stay green (re-run green at Decide).
- **D241 — Honesty line: offline proves DERIVATION + EMISSION + CLI-parse/payload shape; ENFORCEMENT is live-only and rides `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn`.** Proven offline (vendored-source + executed probes): `--allowed-domain` is a cmd-ts multioption (repeated flags accumulate, NO comma-split — vercel 54.13.0 app-BPs0T7SC.mjs:11669-11673); N domains serialize to `{allow:[d1..dN]}` and CANNOT combine with `--network-policy` (throws, :11708-11710); the SDK type declares `allow?: string[]` and the create POST to `/v2/sandboxes` carries the list verbatim; public firewall docs confirm list-of-domains deny-all-except with HTTPS/SNI host-only matching. COMMA-JOIN IS A FOOT-GUN: `--allowed-domain 'a,b'` parses as ONE malformed domain (executed probe) — the emitter must expand to repeated flags, always. NOT provable offline: runtime Firecracker enforcement of a multi-entry list (permit the declared two, block a third) — needs an authenticated create + in-VM probe; it is an ADDENDUM to the existing human gate, never fake-proven. VERSION IS FLOATING: no vercel-CLI pin/provisioning infra exists anywhere in the repo (`VERCEL_BIN` resolves bare `vercel` off PATH); multioption repeatability is SOURCE-proven at 54.13.0 only and is NOT `--help`-probable (the snapshotRetentionFlags idiom detects presence, not repeatability) — drift rides the gate via backlog `connectors-egress-live-enforcement-reconfirm` (p3), not a pin built this wave.
- **D242 — Scope fences.** Widening is a CREATE-TIME, SNAPSHOT-path-only property: the no-snapshot fallback boot stays allow-all (D113(c) unchanged — no wall to widen there) and the `--sandbox-id` reuse path skips `createSandbox` entirely, so a mid-session tool install does NOT widen an existing sandbox until a fresh create (documented freeze; backlog p4 `connectors-egress-reuse-rederive` if ever needed). HTTPS/SNI-only domain filtering noted (both MCP hosts are HTTPS; plain-HTTP hosts cannot be domain-filtered — do not over-promise). knob-4/D148 UNTOUCHED (session-token perms, orthogonal). `connectors-cloud-shim-sdk-auth` (open, unclaimed) orbits the same `createSandbox` function — merge-conflict-only sequencing note for whichever lands second. Off-limits unchanged: theme/paper/golden/deploy_runner/openapi/router.ex.

## Wave 30 — decisions (D243–D248, DECIDED from a 16-report survey + a 5-assignment verify fleet ALL carrying run output; the OFFLINE-RESIDUE + SEAL wave: burn down the offline-provable in-fence defect residue with mutation-proven tests, then name the shortest path to seal; paper `connectors-wave-30-2026-07-21`)

- **D243 [DECIDED — SLICE A IS shim-error-sanitize-belt ONLY; egress-reuse-rederive STAYS LEDGERED — D242 STANDS.]** The V3 verify verdict is LEDGER-ONLY, run-proven: (a) NO offline-provable fix exists — the `--sandbox-id` reuse branch (`cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs` 861-869) skips `createSandbox` entirely, so there is no `--allowed-domain` re-emission to re-derive and no Vercel API to narrow a live sandbox's allowlist without a fresh create (which destroys the /tmp + claude transcript reuse exists to preserve, D134/D137); folding a fix in would MANUFACTURE a mechanism = the speculative build D242 froze. (b) ENFORCEMENT is live-only (D241) — no offline test can prove a stale allowlist causes real egress; a Slice-A test would fake-prove it, the exact trap this epic's 11-task false-done reopening warns against. (c) The leak SELF-HEALS credential-less — `cloud_policy.ex` re-derives BOTH `cloud_mcp_servers/2` and `cloud_egress_hosts/2` from the CURRENT installed set every turn (gated `MapSet.member?(installed, provider)`), and D214 fetches the credential per-turn on BOTH create AND reuse branches, so the turn after an uninstall the credential + MCP entry are already gone; residual capability = one TLS host the same workspace allowlisted seconds earlier, with no credential, self-healing on reap = fail-closed backlog, not IDOR-class. NAMED for the ledger: the INVERSE narrowing-leak (uninstall mid-session leaves a reused sandbox pinned to turn-1's WIDER allowlist) is the one direction D242 never analyzed; re-escalation point stays `connectors-egress-reuse-rederive` (p4) at the live gate — its description is amended with this inverse dimension. Slice A owns ONLY the runner.mjs raw-`res.err` folds at 692/760/781 + the 694 WARNING-`log()` leak, routed through ONE sanitize/truncate helper; credential paths (576/579/609) are ALREADY hardened (fixed-string ERR_CREDENTIAL_FETCH) — do not touch. Provable TODAY via the w12 fake-vercel black-box idiom, zero refactor.
- **D244 [DECIDED — SLICE B vendor contract, from V1 run-proofs against `@chat-adapter/discord@4.34.0` + `chat@4.34.0` .d.ts.]** Component seam is `chat.onAction(handler)` / `onAction(actionIds, handler)`; modal is `chat.onModalSubmit(handler)` (+ `onModalClose` for notifyOnClose modals). There is NO `onComponent`/`onModal` method — the strategic shorthand was directionally right, the vendor names are these. MESSAGE_COMPONENT(3) is acked type-6 DeferredUpdateMessage by the vendor `handleWebhook` (dist index.js:892-896) and forwarded to `chat.processAction` — but reaches a handler ONLY if `onAction` is registered; **the ack is not proof of handling**, so the red-first test must register `onAction` AND assert it fired. MODAL_SUBMIT(5) has NO branch in the vendor dispatch → falls through to `400 Unknown interaction type` (index.js:907); therefore B CANNOT fix modals by registration alone — it must intercept type-5 at Barkpark's `discord.ts` layer (or add a ModalSubmit branch) BEFORE the vendor 400. Ed25519 verify (`verifyKey`) runs BEFORE dispatch (index.js:855-866) → the red-first test signs raw body bytes with a keypair matching `this.publicKey` or it 401s before asserting anything. This worktree has NO `connectors/node_modules` — the builder MUST `npm ci` in `connectors/` (or reference the main-checkout vendor path) before tsc/grep. Extension point is `connectors/test/discord-slash-commands.test.ts` (its Ed25519 + stubbed-fetch harness), NOT `discord-connector.test.ts`. SCOPE NOTE: no first-party bridge surface posts interactive components today (grep: zero ActionRow/ComponentType) — B closes a real protocol/defensive gap (buttons acked-then-dropped), not a live user-facing bug; honest in the ledger.
- **D245 [DECIDED — SLICE C IDOR fix = clean thread-through, THREE workspace-blind callers (V2 corrected the task labels), FAIL-CLOSED nil ruling is load-bearing.]** All four mutations (`rotate`/`pause`/`unpause`/`revoke`) funnel through private `get_ticket_key/1` (keys.ex 200-212) whose WHERE filters only id+kind — the scoping predicate is added ONCE there. Public arities bump to `/2` (thread `workspace_id`). ### Wave 2026-07-21 — Wave 30 DECIDE — offline-residue + seal: four file-disjoint round-1 slices, two cross-tenant security holes among them, egress-reuse held to the ledger by verify, and the SEAL LEDGER naming the 16-child gated remainder

THE THREE DEFECTS ARE CONFIRMED: V1 gave Slice B the real vendor names (`onAction`/`onModalSubmit`, MODAL_SUBMIT 400s at the adapter → needs a discord.ts-layer intercept, ack≠handling) and resolved the node_modules contradiction (present in main checkout, absent here); V2 proved Slice C's harness green today (21/0), a clean 3-caller thread-through, and pinned the FAIL-CLOSED nil ruling (never copy list/1's fail-open nil); the shim-sanitize + keys-IDOR leaks re-confirmed on origin/main. FOUR round-1 disjoint slices: A shim-sanitize (opus), B discord component/modal (FABLE — the one genuinely hard slice), C keys IDOR (opus, waits Elixir), D secrets-audit (opus, waits Elixir). After merge the offline-provable in-fence residue is ZERO; the seal map = 9 human-gate + 7 live-key-cloud (D248). Paper: `connectors-wave-30-2026-07-21`.

FOUR file-disjoint slices ship, all reviewer-verified with gates re-run green on the review branches; the reviewer made ZERO code changes (every slice was already right). Paper `connectors-wave-30-2026-07-21`.

Component (button/select) + modal-submit interactions now reach registered handlers instead of acked-then-dropped: `mountInstall` registers `chat.onAction` + `chat.onModalSubmit` as the 5th/6th thin wrappers on the same `handle` closure, and `extendDiscordInteractionWebhook` wraps `chat.webhooks.discord` in place — byte-identical raw-body forwarding (Ed25519 signs raw bytes), an `onOpenModal` seam POSTing the real type-9 callback, and a MODAL_SUBMIT intercept on the vendor's post-verify 400 that dispatches inside the vendor's ALS store so the reply PATCHes the submit's own token. Reviewer re-ran the gate green: `npm ci` + 12/12 vitest (6 new, incl. the (f) control proving the pre-W30 acked-then-dropped drop) + `tsc --noEmit` clean; the FULL connectors suite (37 files, 542 tests) stays green — no regression from wrapping the discord webhook path for all installs. - **C `connectors-tickets-keys-workspace-blind-idor` — SHIPPED** (final branch `loop-epic/tickets-keys-cross-tenant-idor-workspace-2`, commit `efe719e2c`, PR #5545). No task outside this wave was touched. **CROSS-SLICE:** A/B are Node (scripts + connectors/), C/D are Elixir (api/, file-disjoint — C touches tickets/*, D touches secret_controller+router, no collision); C and D share the same fail-closed cross-tenant idiom (`scope_workspace`/`scope_audit`, both nil→fail-closed) — coherent, no duplicated helper. **LEAD CLOSES on merge:** criterion 2 of each of the four slices (each PR carries a single `Task:` line). **NEXT WAVE — the residue is nearly gone.** Remaining connector-lane defect residue after this merges: `connectors-discord-modal-open-response` (p4, the live modal-OPEN ack-race redesign) and `connectors-egress-reuse-rederive` (p4, D242 documented freeze) — both p4 polish, neither blocks seal. ## Wave 31 — decisions (D249–D253, DECIDED from a 5-report Arm-B survey + 3 verify assignments ALL carrying run output incl. an EXECUTED vendor-mechanics probe modeling the whole hold-and-substitute design; the DEFECT-RESIDUE wave: W30's modal honest ceiling gets its redesign, the funnel lockstep gets a red-on-revert tripwire, and every row already covered on main closes/amends honestly; paper `connectors-wave-31-2026-07-22`; **Arm: B**)

- **D249 [DECIDED — B1 modal HOLD-AND-SUBSTITUTE ratified from V2's executed probe against the VENDORED `chat@4.34.0` + `@chat-adapter/discord@4.34.0` dists in the real Ed25519 harness.]** (1) `extendDiscordInteractionWebhook` gains an options argument (`{ modalWindowMs }`) at its single call site (`index.ts:489`); the wrapper passes its OWN `waitUntil` into the vendor call — the vendor creates + registers the dispatch task synchronously BEFORE constructing the ack Response (dist:898-905; chat:2753-2763; V2 `[a] dispatchCaptured=true handlerCompleted=false`) — and forwards captured tasks on to the caller's real `waitUntil` (webhook-server.ts:626-666 supplies one today). (2) `onOpenModal` becomes SIGNAL-ONLY on the webhook path — NO network POST (the W30 raced type-9 callback fetch is deleted); the wrapper races {openModal-called, dispatch-settled, deadline} while HOLDING the vendor's type-5 ack; a modal win discards the held ack and answers type-9 `{type:9, data: modal}` as THE interaction HTTP response. Structurally safe — the vendor ack only ever reaches Discord as our own return value; V2 proved ZERO prior egress at the substitution instant (`[b] egressAtModalOpen=0`, no callback POST, no @original PATCH) → no 40060 possible; the race is abolished, not merely won. (3) Non-modal handlers pay min(dispatch-settle, window) — never more. (4) Knob = process-global env `CONNECTORS_DISCORD_MODAL_WINDOW_MS` on `BridgeConfig` (the `intFromEnv` pattern — the ONLY config idiom that exists; S2 grep-proved NO per-install config seam anywhere: `connector_installs` = 6 columns, `ConnectorInstall` has no config field); default **250ms**, clamped to [0..2500] (well under Discord's 3s), **0 disables the hold** (vendor ack returns immediately; openModal keeps the W30 warn+undefined path) — Rival A's kernel honored: near-zero cost when unused. Default + clamp + 0-disable all test-pinned. (5) MIXED-REPLY IS FIXED, NOT CEILINGED: on a modal win, `onOpenModal` — which runs inside the vendor's `requestContext` ALS continuation — flips `slashCommand.initialResponseSent = true`; V2 proved the store reachable AND the flip observable in vendor ROUTING (`[c] @original-PATCH-count=0 POST-followup-count=1` vs control `PATCH=1`) so a same-turn `event.channel.post` goes out as a POST follow-up instead of PATCHing an `@original` that never exists after type-9. The flip MUST live inside `onOpenModal` (outside the ALS context `getStore()` is undefined — V2 risk note); reuse the exact `adapter as unknown as {requestContext:…}` cast discord.ts:694 already carries or tsc reds. (6) openModal AFTER the window: warn+undefined, never silent success — the honest-ceiling law continues. (7) WEBHOOK-ONLY: the gateway path calls `handleApplicationCommandInteraction(context, flags)` with NO options (dist:1163 — no waitUntil, no onOpenModal); gateway openModal stays a documented ceiling → backlog `connectors-discord-gateway-modal-hold` (p4), NOT this wave. (8) Every new behavior red-first + mutation-proven in the existing raw-bytes harness (V1 pinned the baseline: fresh origin/main `289187318` gates green — 542/542 vitest, tsc clean, test (h) at discord-slash-commands.test.ts:558-568).
- **D250 [DECIDED — egress-reuse: D243(a) AMENDED on a V3 run-proof; (b)/(c) STAND; still NO BUILD.]** D243(a)'s clause "no Vercel API to narrow a live sandbox's allowlist without a fresh create" is FALSE: `vercel sandbox config network-policy <name> [--allowed-domain …|--allowed-cidr …|--denied-cidr …|--mode …]` ships in vercel CLI 54.13.0 — the version VERIFIED ON THE SURVEY/VERIFY MACHINE, not repo-pinned (V3: no pin artifact exists anywhere; connectors.yml uses a hermetic fake vercel) — and its vendored handler (app-BPs0T7SC.mjs:15718-15749) resolves an EXISTING sandbox by name via `sandboxClient.get` and mutates it in place with `await sandbox.update({ networkPolicy })` — no create, no snapshot, /tmp + transcript preserved. D243(b) (enforcement is live-only, D241) and D243(c) (credential-less per-turn self-heal) are untouched and stand. The runner's reuse-skip-create branch is byte-identical since STRICTLY BEFORE D243 (last touch #4184/W29; the sole post-D243 commit #5552 touched only sanitize call sites — V3 full-diff read). RULING: amend the task description (strike the no-API clause, name the update API), keep p4/open, re-escalate at the live gate `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn` — stop/resume semantics (does `update()` work on a stopped/auto-resuming sandbox? already-open connections?) are live-only and out of Arm-B scope. No wave slice.
- **D251 [DECIDED — `connectors-shim-error-sanitize-belt` CLOSED at Decide with merge evidence; main is COHERENT.]** The wish's item-4 census (L1) resolves to exactly the two named discord rows plus this one stale open row — nothing else W30-filed is open+executable. The two-PR anomaly is benign: #5543 (5ec9cd698) is a ZERO-DIFF duplicate merge whose SOLE PARENT is #5552 (1720a9965) — `git diff 1720a9965 5ec9cd698` is empty; that is why per-path history shows only #5552. 3 `sanitizeErr()` call sites (L723/791/812) cover all 4 D243-named leak surfaces — the 694-era WARNING log reuses L723's already-sanitized `msg` by construction (3 = call sites, 4 = surfaces, no residual gap). S4 RAN `scripts/connectors/w30-shim-sanitize-proof.sh` offline: exit 0, 4/4 scenarios (create / npm-install / version-strict / version-warn-694). connectors.yml wires the proof exactly once. Closed by the lead at Decide; no residual slice.
- **D252 [DECIDED — B2 = mountInstall DRIVER TEST, not a shared symbol; file-disjoint from B1 by construction.]** The D232 funnel exists in exactly two places (S3 repo-wide grep: `src/index.ts:420-427` production + `test/discord-slash-commands.test.ts:139` mirrored harness copy). Mechanism: a NEW test file drives the REAL exported `mountInstall` (precedent `ack-first-indicator.test.ts`'s mountRealInstall and its explicit vacuous-green rationale — a shared-symbol unit test stays green if the wrapper reverts to `event.text` while the unused symbol survives; the driver test exercises the literal production path). ### Wave 2026-07-23 — Wave 34 DECIDE — winning-recipe throughput wave (E6+E7): the D263 trio adjudicated with a 5-assignment verify fleet ALL carrying run output — deploy-install + SettingsLive belt BUILD (round 1, file-disjoint), ChatLive belt REFUTED by rule-grounded verification and closed `cancelled` (the scoreboard's premise-failure-caught metric)

**Arm: E (E6+E7)** (research-program /papers/epic-cycle-research-program-abcde; parent research epic `task-09f4775e7ccc2cca`; paper `connectors-wave-34-2026-07-23`). Premise smoke green at every phase: deploy-grep zero-hit on origin/main; claude_chat.ex sites at :136/:218/:1283/:1288 (the task's old ":159" citation was stale — corrected in the brief); runner.mjs SCOPE at :81, TIMEOUT backstop at :84 (the wish's ":79" was a pre-existing citation slip). Decisions D264–D269:

- **D264 [SCOPE — W34 builds 2 of the D263 trio; the ChatLive belt is REFUTED and closed `cancelled`.]** The rule-grounding verifier's per-handler matrix (all 20 state-changing handlers, file:line) rules LEAVE UNGATED across the board: (a) the scoped route is already sealed at mount by W26-1 `{LiveAuth,:scoped_admin}` (chat_live_scoped_admin_test.exs proves outsider rejection); (b) the flat `/studio/chat` route is a deliberate `:admin` superuser surface where StudioChrome PINS `current_workspace` to the seeded Default (studio_chrome.ex:454-465 — NOT nil as the digest assumed), so a per-workspace belt would gate an arbitrary pinned workspace that is never the write target, and would REFUSE legitimate global-admin tokens whose home workspace != Default (auth.ex:388-392; workspace_admin?/2 is role-only and never folds global perms, tenancy/auth.ex:337-339); (c) session archive/delete/unarchive/rename are the documented `:global` superuser path (D17/D18, chat_live.ex:936-938) — gating them per-owner-workspace is a behavior change, not hardening; (d) every turn write derives its workspace from the SESSION's persisted owner_workspace_id, explicitly "NEVER the ambient current_workspace" (chat_live.ex:4005-4020), so a current_workspace belt protects nothing at risk. Option B (a GLOBAL-admin re-affirm on arm-bypass/session-delete only) is DECLINED as marginal: arm-bypass already carries the D48/D55 typed-confirm ceremony and its danger fires only on the session's own next spawn; its non-vacuous test story was also never proven for ChatLive's dual-mount shape. `connectors-chatlive-per-write-belt` closes `cancelled` with this evidence — W34's premise-failure-caught data point. RE-OPEN TRIGGER: only if ChatLive ever gains a handler whose write target IS the ambient current_workspace.

- **D265 [INSTALL SHAPE — a wrapper exec'ing barkpark-node; NEVER the env-node shebang; `:sandbox_runner` config stays unset.]** L1 /proc-proof against the live guerrilla BEAM: PATH=`/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin:/usr/games:/usr/local/games:/snap/bin`; `node` does NOT resolve on it (`which node` exit 1) and `barkpark.service` carries no `Environment=` override — so a plain copy of the runner relying on its own `#!/usr/bin/env node` ships as a SILENT NO-OP (the strategist's feared failure mode one layer down: it is NODE that is unfindable, not the runner). The digest's "asdf shim walk-up" hypothesis is REFUTED. DECIDED shape: deploy copies the checkout's `scripts/connectors/cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs` to `/usr/local/bin/cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs` AND installs a 2-line wrapper `/usr/local/bin/cloud-sandbox-runner` → `exec /usr/local/bin/barkpark-node /usr/local/bin/cloud-sandbox-runner.mjs "$@"` (barkpark-node v26.5.0 is a real dependency-free ELF on the box, proven to run .mjs with argv passthrough), 0755, re-copied every deploy (versioned-with-checkout, the #3374 copy idiom), guarded LOUD non-fatal in the bp-CLI-install style. ClaudeChat `:sandbox_runner` config stays UNSET — the bare-name default (`claude_chat.ex:136/:218`) matches the wrapper basename and System.find_executable resolves `/usr/local/bin` (the documented /proc-proven `bp` precedent, instance-deploy.sh:896-913).

- **D266 [MUTATION-PROOF NON-VACUITY LAW for install steps.]** instance-deploy_test.sh is PROVEN BLIND to the bp-CLI install block — disabling that whole block leaves 176/176 PASS exit 0, byte-identical to baseline — so the delete-the-line recipe would FALSE-GREEN a runner install written in that style. LAW: every new install step lands WITH its own explicit harness checks; for this runner they must assert (a) wrapper present + executable, (b) wrapper CONTENT names the exec target `/usr/local/bin/barkpark-node` (file-presence alone would green a shebang-only no-op — the D265 failure mode), (c) installed .mjs content matches the checkout source (cmp), (d) idempotent redeploy. Mutation-proof = delete the install lines → exactly the new checks red (all others green) → restore → green; BOTH outputs captured as evidence. (Context: main has no branch protection — deploy-harnesses.yml fires on deploy/** PRs but green is discipline-only; the reviewer records run URLs.)

- **D267 [PREFLIGHT — standalone script; `vercel sandbox list --scope guerrilla` is the entitlement-grade probe.]** Live empirics (vercel CLI 54.13.0): `whoami` ~4.6s green, bogus token fails ~2.8s exit 1 non-interactive; `whoami --scope guerrilla` ~3.9s green and a bad/inaccessible scope fails ~2-3s ("scope does not exist" — cannot distinguish not-exist from no-access, fine for preflight); **`vercel sandbox list --scope guerrilla` ~3.3s green — non-creating, round-trips the real Sandbox API** (proves team + Sandbox entitlement, strictly closer to what create checks; bad scope fails ~2.2s HTTP 403). None approaches the 10m create backstop (runner.mjs:84) — the stall lives ONLY in `sandbox create`. DECIDED: new `scripts/connectors/preflight-vercel.sh` chains (1) `command -v vercel` (2) `vercel whoami` (3) `vercel sandbox list --scope guerrilla` (4) ANTHROPIC_API_KEY presence (d10-messages-api-ttfb.sh:144 honest-refusal idiom) — each failure LOUD, names the missing prereq, exit 1; never invokes `sandbox create`. Portability: never shell bare `timeout` (macOS dev boxes have neither `timeout` nor `gtimeout` — guard with command -v or document Linux-only). CI: connectors.yml hardcodes proof scripts BY NAME — the preflight's offline self-test (fake-vercel shim: whoami-fail / list-fail / key-unset modes) gets its own named step or it never runs in CI. Residual create-time risk (quota/concurrency/Hobby 30-day pause) stays on `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn`. Docs land in `scripts/connectors/README.md` (gate-free, already canonical for this code path) + optional pointer in deploy/README.md — NEVER PROD_OPS.md (5990/6000B, effectively full) and NO new card (7/7 hard cap).

- **D268 [BELT ORACLE LAW — the STATE assertion is the only proven non-vacuous oracle; the bare "owner or admin of this workspace" substring is FORBIDDEN.]** Run-proven red/green at verify: neutering only the re-gate (settings_live.ex:427 → `true`) flips a mid-socket-downgrade probe RED at the STATE assertion while the flash-substring assertion STILL PASSES — the phrase also lives in STATIC help text at settings_live.ex:622, so `html =~ "owner or admin of this workspace"` is vacuous. The proven mechanic (transfers to any per-write belt test): mount as workspace-admin → direct `Repo.update` of the workspace_memberships row admin→member (NO public revoke API exists; workspace_admin? → membership/2 does a FRESH Repo.one per call, auth.ex:142-152, zero socket cache, so the downgrade is seen at click time) → render_click on the STILL-MOUNTED view → REQUIRED oracle: the targeted settings UNCHANGED (e.g. `Tenancy.workspace_chat_settings/1`, theme, plugin overrides). Optional flash oracle: the refusal-unique prefix "You need to be an owner or admin" (:425, absent from static text), and/or assert the success phrase ABSENT. Also proven: local `mix test` IS feasible in a worktree (symlink `deps` from the primary checkout, one ~13min cold `MIX_ENV=test mix compile`, then the file runs ~40s; Postgres on localhost:5432).

- **D269 [CENSUS + BACKLOG.]** Dispatch-fresh census clean: no commit newer than feedcb981 touches the belt files; among 10 open PRs only #5937 overlaps and only via studio_live/shared.ex (file-disjoint from both belts); Elixir gate GREEN (3 success / 4 superseded-cancels / 0 red in the last 8 main push runs) — the direction-debate's gate-contention attack is refuted by measurement, so the belts dispatch by rule. ### Wave 2026-08-17 — Wave 35 DECIDE — the first live breath, made drivable: the committed harnesses PROVABLY cannot close the live gates (w11 is 401-locked, w14 is hermetic), the crown is credential-conditional exactly as predicted, so the wave builds the missing DRIVER + the ONE human packet, seals a real cross-tenant credential escalation, and closes five ledger rows by evidence

**Wave 35** (paper `connectors-wave-35-2026-08-17`; 14 survey lanes + 8 verify assignments, every one reported, zero coverage deficit; premise smoke green on every load-bearing citation — D265/D266/D260/D33/D34/D60/D110/D132/D137-D139/D142/D190 all git-shown and covering). Two strategize-era contradictions RESOLVED by verification, one premise stack refuted, one live security finding confirmed. Decisions D270–D278:

- **D270 [THE CROWN NEEDS A NEW DRIVER — w11/w14 structurally cannot close the live-success gates; `scripts/connectors/w35-live-turn-driver.sh` is the wave's headline build.]** Run-proven: w11-isolated-turn-proof.sh injects `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY="$BOGUS_KEY"` (line 181) OVERRIDING any real key and hard-asserts `is_error:true`/`EXPECT_STATUS=401` (lines 48/215) — a real-key SUCCESS fails its own assertions; w14-session-sandbox-proof.sh is hermetic by design ("provisions NO real sandbox", fake vercel). Neither committed harness can close `connectors-hg-live-isolated-cloud-turn` or `connectors-hg-live-cloud-multiturn` despite their task prose naming w11. DECIDED: build a bounded driver with four modes — bake (warm snapshot with the pinned claude 2.1.211; registry-checked BEFORE create), single (one stream-json turn, assert result frame is_error absent/false, cloud_policy belt argv mirrored), multiturn (turn-1 `--keep-sandbox` + `--session-id`, harvest the `bp_sandbox created` frame, turn-2 `--sandbox-id` + `--resume` — the shim reuses only the FILESYSTEM; conversation recall is claude's session flags, which w14 never pinned), teardown (EXIT-trap remove + `vercel sandbox snapshots delete` + end-of-run ls/snapshots-ls-empty asserts; the delete verb confirmed live in CLI 54.13.0). Key custody stays D110: driver's own env → the shim's single `--env` pair, never argv/logs (W30 belt); hermetic `--self-test` (fake-vercel argv recorder) with an internal mutation leg wired as a NAMED connectors.yml step. EGRESS HONESTY: the deny-all lock rides ONLY the runner's snapshot branch (runner.mjs ~742-785) and ZERO snapshots exist in the account — bake-first is the rule; a snapshot-less live turn must WARN allow-all loudly, never silently.
- **D271 [GATE-CONTRACT RATIFICATION — a shim-driven turn satisfies the two hg live gates' LETTER; the crux's workspace-token clause stays OPEN.]** The isolated-turn gate's criteria say "Harness live run" generically (guerrilla scope, teardown, no-key-persist — no workspace/profile requirement in any criterion); the multiturn gate's one beyond-shim clause is "SAME Barkpark session", AMENDED per the D190 lead-reword precedent to session continuity via `--session-id`/`--resume` on one driver session (recorded here so the sitting is not re-litigated). Crux `task-fe78ccb664308739` crit-3 ("workspace-scoped chat+workspace_id token drives the turn") is UNSATISFIABLE in-sandbox by design (BARKPARK_* never crosses, runner.mjs:163-177) — it stays OPEN pending either a D190 amendment by the lead or the full Elixir-path sitting; the shim sitting does NOT close it. The execution_profile mechanism EXISTS on origin/main (workspace chat settings, claude_chat.ex:209-212) — the census "no storage" was a no-row-SET condition, one settings write away, NOT a missing table.
- **D272 [PUBLIC ROUTE PREMISE REFUTED — the Caddy /connectors route is ARMED and green; NO route slice.]** Live probes 2026-08-17: public `https://guerrilla.barkpark.cloud/connectors/health` = 200 end-to-end; the `/connectors/webhooks/slack` 404 is the bridge's own DELIBERATE opaque failure for an unmounted install (reproduced byte-identically over loopback :4020, excluding Caddy; webhook-server.ts parseRoute accepts the path then null-install → notFound), and `/mcp` 405 is Phoenix :4000, not the bridge. `arm_caddy_connectors_route` is defined (instance-deploy.sh:584), invoked (:636), and its output is in the live Caddyfile.
